// src/server.js
// LinguaWave - Main Server Entry Point
// Uses Node.js core modules + Express + Socket.IO

'use strict';
require('dotenv').config();
const http = require('http');           // Node.js core: HTTP module
const path = require('path');           // Node.js core: Path module
const { EventEmitter } = require('events'); // Node.js core: EventEmitter



const app = require('./app');
const { initSocket } = require('./sockets/socketManager');
const { connectFirebase } = require('./config/firebase');
const logger = require('./utils/logger');

// ─── Server EventEmitter (local module pattern) ───────────────────────────────
class ServerEvents extends EventEmitter {
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.setMaxListeners(20);
  }
}
const serverEvents = new ServerEvents();

// ─── Create HTTP Server manually (Node.js core) ───────────────────────────────
const server = http.createServer(app);

// ─── Initialize Socket.IO ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const io = initSocket(server);
app.set('io', io); // Make io accessible in routes

// ─── Bootstrap function ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function bootstrap() {
  try {
    // Connect the (single) database
    await connectFirebase();
    serverEvents.emit('db:firebase:connected');

    const PORT = process.env.PORT || 5000; // matches the frontend's default API URL (Render injects PORT)

    server.listen(PORT, () => {
      logger.info(`🚀 LinguaWave server running on port ${PORT}`);
      logger.info(`📡 Environment: ${process.env.NODE_ENV}`);
      logger.info(`🔗 API Base: http://localhost:${PORT}/api/v1`);
      serverEvents.emit('server:ready', PORT);
    });

  } catch (error) {
    logger.error('Failed to bootstrap server:', error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

// ─── EventEmitter Listeners ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
serverEvents.on('db:firebase:connected', () => logger.info('✅ Firebase Firestore connected'));
serverEvents.on('server:ready', (port) => logger.info(`✅ Server ready on ${port}`));

// ─── Graceful Shutdown ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const shutdown = (signal) => {
  logger.info(`\n${signal} received. Graceful shutdown initiated...`);
  server.close(() => {
    logger.info('HTTP server closed');
    process.exit(0);
  });
};

process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'));
process.on('SIGINT', () => shutdown('SIGINT'));
process.on('uncaughtException', (err) => {
  logger.error('Uncaught Exception:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});
process.on('unhandledRejection', (reason) => {
  logger.error('Unhandled Rejection:', reason);
});

bootstrap();

module.exports = { server, serverEvents };

FILE: backend/src/sockets/socketManager.js
========================================================================
// src/sockets/socketManager.js
// Socket.IO Real-Time Communication Manager
// Handles: chat, pronunciation feedback, live session updates

'use strict';

const { Server } = require('socket.io');
const { verifyAccessToken } = require('../utils/jwtHelper');
const User = require('../models/firestore/User');
const ChatMessage = require('../models/firestore/ChatMessage');
const logger = require('../utils/logger');
const { originCallback } = require('../config/cors');

const isValidRoom = (r) => typeof r === 'string' && /^[\w-]{1,40}$/.test(r);

// In-memory store of connected users (use Redis in production)
const connectedUsers = new Map(); // userId -> Set of socketIds

/**
 * Initialize Socket.IO server with JWT authentication
 * @param {http.Server} httpServer
 * @returns {Server} io
 */
function initSocket(httpServer) {
  const io = new Server(httpServer, {
    cors: {
      // Same policy as the REST API. (Previously: ''.split(',') => [''] which blocked localhost in dev.)
      origin: originCallback,
      credentials: true,
    },
    pingTimeout: 60000,
    pingInterval: 25000,
  });

  // ─── Socket.IO JWT Middleware ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  io.use(async (socket, next) => {
    try {
      const token =
        socket.handshake.auth?.token ||
        socket.handshake.headers.authorization?.split(' ')[1];

      if (!token) {
        return next(new Error('Authentication required'));
      }

      const decoded = await verifyAccessToken(token);
      const found = await User.findById(decoded.id);

      if (!found) return next(new Error('User not found'));

      socket.user = { id: found.id, username: found.username, role: found.role };
      next();
    } catch (err) {
      logger.warn(`Socket auth failed: ${err.message}`);
      next(new Error('Authentication failed'));
    }
  });

  // ─── Connection Handler ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  io.on('connection', (socket) => {
    const { user } = socket;
    logger.info(`Socket connected: ${user.username} (${socket.id})`);

    // Track connected user
    if (!connectedUsers.has(user.id)) {
      connectedUsers.set(user.id, new Set());
    }
    connectedUsers.get(user.id).add(socket.id);

    // Join personal room for targeted messages
    socket.join(`user:${user.id}`);

    // Broadcast updated online count
    io.emit('users:online', connectedUsers.size);

    // ── Chat Events ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    // Join a chat room
    socket.on('chat:join', (roomId) => {
      if (!isValidRoom(roomId)) return;
      socket.join(`room:${roomId}`);
      socket.to(`room:${roomId}`).emit('chat:userJoined', {
        userId: user.id,
        username: user.username,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      });
      logger.info(`${user.username} joined room: ${roomId}`);
    });

    // Leave a chat room
    socket.on('chat:leave', (roomId) => {
      if (!isValidRoom(roomId)) return;
      socket.leave(`room:${roomId}`);
      socket.to(`room:${roomId}`).emit('chat:userLeft', {
        userId: user.id,
        username: user.username,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      });
    });

    // Send chat message (real-time, saved via REST API separately)
    socket.on('chat:message', async (data) => {
      try {
        const { content, roomId = 'global' } = data || {};
        if (typeof content !== 'string' || !content.trim() || !isValidRoom(roomId)) return;
        // Only members of the room may post to it
        if (!socket.rooms.has(`room:${roomId}`)) socket.join(`room:${roomId}`);

        // Save message to Firestore
        const message = await ChatMessage.create({
          userId: user.id,
          username: user.username,
          content: content.trim().substring(0, 2000),
          roomId,
          type: 'TEXT',
        });

        // Broadcast to room
        io.to(`room:${roomId}`).emit('chat:message', {
          id: message.id,
          userId: message.userId,
          content: message.content,
          roomId: message.roomId,
          type: message.type,
          user: message.user,
          createdAt: message.createdAt,
        });

      } catch (err) {
        logger.error('Socket chat:message error:', err);
        socket.emit('chat:error', { message: 'Failed to send message' });
      }
    });

    // Typing indicator
    socket.on('chat:typing', ({ roomId, isTyping } = {}) => {
      if (!isValidRoom(roomId)) return;
      socket.to(`room:${roomId}`).emit('chat:typing', {
        userId: user.id,
        username: user.username,
        isTyping,
      });
    });

    // ── Practice Session Events ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    // Start a practice session
    socket.on('session:start', (data = {}) => {
      if (!data.sessionId) return;
      socket.join(`session:${data.sessionId}`);
      socket.emit('session:started', {
        sessionId: data.sessionId,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      });
      logger.info(`${user.username} started session: ${data.sessionId}`);
    });

    // Real-time pronunciation feedback during session
    socket.on('pronunciation:feedback', (data) => {
      // Broadcast feedback to user's personal room (multi-device support)
      io.to(`user:${user.id}`).emit('pronunciation:feedback', {
        ...(data && typeof data === 'object' ? data : {}),
        timestamp: new Date(),
      });
    });

    // ── Disconnect ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    socket.on('disconnect', (reason) => {
      const userSockets = connectedUsers.get(user.id);
      if (userSockets) {
        userSockets.delete(socket.id);
        if (userSockets.size === 0) {
          connectedUsers.delete(user.id);
        }
      }

      io.emit('users:online', connectedUsers.size);
      logger.info(`Socket disconnected: ${user.username} (${reason})`);
    });

    socket.on('error', (err) => {
      logger.error(`Socket error for ${user.username}:`, err);
    });
  });

  logger.info('Socket.IO initialized');
  return io;
}

/**
 * Get online user count
 */
function getOnlineCount() {
  return connectedUsers.size;
}

/**
 * Check if user is online
 */
function isUserOnline(userId) {
  return connectedUsers.has(userId);
}

module.exports = { initSocket, getOnlineCount, isUserOnline };

FILE: backend/src/utils/fetchRetry.js
========================================================================
const logger = require('./logger');

// Retries only on network-level failures (dropped connection, reset, timeout).
// HTTP responses (401, 429, 500...) are returned as-is for the caller to handle.
async function fetchRetry(url, options = {}, retries = 2, delayMs = 800) {
  let lastErr;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= retries; attempt++) {
    try {
      return await fetch(url, { ...options, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(45000) });
    } catch (err) {
      lastErr = err;
      const cause = err.cause?.code || err.cause?.message || err.message;
      logger.warn(`[fetchRetry] attempt ${attempt + 1}/${retries + 1} failed: ${cause}`);
      if (attempt < retries) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, delayMs * (attempt + 1)));
    }
  }
  throw lastErr;
}

module.exports = fetchRetry;

FILE: backend/src/utils/fileStream.js
========================================================================
// src/utils/fileStream.js
// File operations using Node.js fs module, streams, and zlib compression

'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const zlib = require('zlib');
const { pipeline, Transform } = require('stream');
const { promisify } = require('util');
const logger = require('./logger');

const pipelineAsync = promisify(pipeline);

/**
 * Compress a file using gzip (zlib + streams)
 * @param {string} inputPath
 * @param {string} outputPath
 * @returns {Promise<void>}
 */
async function compressFile(inputPath, outputPath) {
  const readStream = fs.createReadStream(inputPath);
  const writeStream = fs.createWriteStream(outputPath);
  const gzip = zlib.createGzip({ level: 6 });

  await pipelineAsync(readStream, gzip, writeStream);
  logger.info(`Compressed: ${inputPath} → ${outputPath}`);
}

/**
 * Decompress a gzip file
 */
async function decompressFile(inputPath, outputPath) {
  const readStream = fs.createReadStream(inputPath);
  const writeStream = fs.createWriteStream(outputPath);
  const gunzip = zlib.createGunzip();

  await pipelineAsync(readStream, gunzip, writeStream);
  logger.info(`Decompressed: ${inputPath} → ${outputPath}`);
}

/**
 * Read file using callback pattern (traditional Node.js)
 */
function readFileCallback(filePath, callback) {
  fs.readFile(filePath, 'utf8', (err, data) => {
    if (err) return callback(err);
    callback(null, data);
  });
}

/**
 * Read file using Promise (modern pattern)
 */
const readFileAsync = (filePath) => {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    fs.readFile(filePath, 'utf8', (err, data) => {
      if (err) reject(err);
      else resolve(data);
    });
  });
};

/**
 * Write JSON data to file (async/await)
 */
async function writeJsonFile(filePath, data) {
  const dir = path.dirname(filePath);
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  }
  await fs.promises.writeFile(filePath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), 'utf8');
}

/**
 * Compress audio data buffer (for storing recordings)
 * Uses zlib deflate
 */
function compressBuffer(buffer) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    zlib.deflate(buffer, (err, compressed) => {
      if (err) reject(err);
      else resolve(compressed);
    });
  });
}

/**
 * Decompress buffer
 */
function decompressBuffer(buffer) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    zlib.inflate(buffer, (err, decompressed) => {
      if (err) reject(err);
      else resolve(decompressed);
    });
  });
}

/**
 * Stream-based file stats reporter (Transform stream example)
 */
class StatsTransform extends Transform {
  constructor(options = {}) {
    super(options);
    this.bytesProcessed = 0;
    this.chunks = 0;
  }

  _transform(chunk, encoding, callback) {
    this.bytesProcessed += chunk.length;
    this.chunks++;
    this.push(chunk);
    callback();
  }

  _flush(callback) {
    logger.info(`Stream stats: ${this.chunks} chunks, ${this.bytesProcessed} bytes`);
    callback();
  }
}

module.exports = {
  compressFile,
  decompressFile,
  readFileCallback,
  readFileAsync,
  writeJsonFile,
  compressBuffer,
  decompressBuffer,
  StatsTransform,
};

FILE: backend/src/utils/jwtHelper.js
========================================================================
// src/utils/jwtHelper.js
// JWT helpers — backed by the Firestore `authSessions` collection.
//
// Flow:
//   Login / Register:
//     generateTokenPair() → creates AuthSession doc in Firestore
//
//   Every API request:
//     verifyAccessToken()  → fast, stateless JWT check only
//
//   Token refresh:
//     verifyRefreshToken() → JWT check + Firestore AuthSession lookup
//     rotateRefreshToken() → atomic rotate in Firestore
//
//   Logout:
//     invalidateSession()  → deletes AuthSession doc from Firestore
//     invalidateAllSessions() → deletes ALL docs for user (logout-all)

'use strict';

const jwt         = require('jsonwebtoken');
const crypto      = require('crypto');
const AuthSession = require('../models/firestore/AuthSession');
const logger      = require('./logger');
const { AppError } = require('../middleware/errorMiddleware');
const { ACCESS_SECRET, REFRESH_SECRET, ACCESS_TTL, REFRESH_TTL } = require('../config/env');

// ── Hash helper — we never store raw refresh tokens anywhere ─────────────────
const hashToken = (raw) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(raw).digest('hex');

// ── Token pair generation + Firestore session creation ────────────────────────────
/**
 * Generate an access + refresh token pair and persist the refresh token
 * hash to Firestore so the server can invalidate it later.
 *
 * @param {object} user       User document (needs id, email, role)
 * @param {object} [meta]     Optional metadata { userAgent, ipAddress, provider }
 * @returns {{ accessToken, refreshToken }}
 */
const generateTokenPair = async (user, meta = {}) => {
  const payload = { 
    id: user.id, 
    email: user.email, 
    role: user.role,
    jti: crypto.randomUUID() 
  };

  const accessToken  = jwt.sign(payload, ACCESS_SECRET,  { expiresIn: ACCESS_TTL });
  const refreshToken = jwt.sign(payload, REFRESH_SECRET, { expiresIn: REFRESH_TTL });

  // Persist hashed refresh token to Firestore
  await AuthSession.createSession({
    userId:    user.id,
    tokenHash: hashToken(refreshToken),
    userAgent: meta.userAgent  || '',
    ipAddress: meta.ipAddress  || '',
    provider:  meta.provider   || 'email',
  });

  return { accessToken, refreshToken };
};

// ── Access token verification (stateless) ─────────────────────────────────────
const verifyAccessToken = (token) => {
  // We no longer catch the error here, we let the middleware handle TokenExpiredError
  // or JsonWebTokenError so it can return appropriate 401s instead of 500s.
  return jwt.verify(token, ACCESS_SECRET);
};

// ── Refresh token verification + Firestore session lookup ─────────────────────────
/**
 * Verifies the JWT signature AND checks that the token exists (not revoked)
 * in Firestore.
 *
 * @param {string} rawToken   The raw refresh token from the httpOnly cookie
 * @returns {object}          Decoded JWT payload
 * @throws {Error}            If invalid, expired, or revoked
 */
const verifyRefreshToken = async (rawToken) => {
  // 1. Verify JWT signature
  let decoded;
  try {
    decoded = jwt.verify(rawToken, REFRESH_SECRET);
  } catch (err) {
    throw new AppError('Invalid or expired refresh token', 401, 'INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN');
  }

  // 2. Check against Firestore (catches revoked / logged-out sessions)
  const session = await AuthSession.findByToken(hashToken(rawToken));
  if (!session) {
    logger.warn(`Refresh token not found in Firestore for user ${decoded.id} — possible token reuse attack`);
    // If the token existed once but was rotated away AND someone tries to use
    // the old value, revoke ALL sessions for that user (replay attack defense).
    await AuthSession.deleteAllForUser(decoded.id);
    throw new AppError('Session expired or revoked. Please log in again.', 401, 'SESSION_REVOKED');
  }

  return decoded;
};

// ── Token rotation (called on every successful refresh) ───────────────────────
/**
 * Atomically swap old refresh token hash for new one in Firestore.
 * Returns { accessToken, refreshToken }.
 */
const rotateTokens = async (user, oldRawToken, meta = {}) => {
  const payload = { 
    id: user.id, 
    email: user.email, 
    role: user.role,
    jti: crypto.randomUUID()
  };

  const newAccessToken  = jwt.sign(payload, ACCESS_SECRET,  { expiresIn: ACCESS_TTL });
  const newRefreshToken = jwt.sign(payload, REFRESH_SECRET, { expiresIn: REFRESH_TTL });

  // Rotate in Firestore — keeps prevTokenHash for 30s grace window
  const rotated = await AuthSession.rotateToken(hashToken(oldRawToken), hashToken(newRefreshToken));

  if (!rotated) {
    // Race condition or token already rotated — issue brand new session
    logger.warn(`Rotation miss for user ${user.id} — creating new session`);
    await AuthSession.createSession({
      userId:    user.id,
      tokenHash: hashToken(newRefreshToken),
      userAgent: meta.userAgent || '',
      ipAddress: meta.ipAddress || '',
      provider:  meta.provider  || 'email',
    });
  }

  return { accessToken: newAccessToken, refreshToken: newRefreshToken };
};

// ── Session invalidation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const invalidateSession    = (rawToken)  => AuthSession.deleteByToken(hashToken(rawToken));
const invalidateAllSessions = (userId)   => AuthSession.deleteAllForUser(userId);
const getActiveSessions    = (userId)    => AuthSession.getActiveSessions(userId);

module.exports = {
  generateTokenPair,
  verifyAccessToken,
  verifyRefreshToken,
  rotateTokens,
  invalidateSession,
  invalidateAllSessions,
  getActiveSessions,
  hashToken,
};

// src/utils/logger.js
// Winston Logger - structured logging utility

'use strict';

const winston = require('winston');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');

// Render (and most PaaS hosts) collect stdout and have an ephemeral disk, so in production we log to the
// console only. Set LOG_TO_FILE=true to force file logging anywhere.
const useFiles = process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production' || process.env.LOG_TO_FILE === 'true';
const logsDir = path.join(__dirname, '../../logs');
if (useFiles && !fs.existsSync(logsDir)) {
  fs.mkdirSync(logsDir, { recursive: true });
}

const { combine, timestamp, printf, colorize, errors } = winston.format;

// Custom log format
const logFormat = printf(({ level, message, timestamp, stack }) => {
  return `${timestamp} [${level}]: ${stack || message}`;
});

const logger = winston.createLogger({
  level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL || 'info',
  format: combine(
    timestamp({ format: 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss' }),
    errors({ stack: true }),
    logFormat
  ),
  transports: [
    // Console transport with color
    new winston.transports.Console({
      format: combine(
        colorize({ all: true }),
        timestamp({ format: 'HH:mm:ss' }),
        logFormat
      ),
    }),
    ...(useFiles ? [
      new winston.transports.File({
        filename: path.join(logsDir, 'error.log'),
        level: 'error',
        maxsize: 5 * 1024 * 1024, // 5MB
        maxFiles: 5,
      }),
      new winston.transports.File({
        filename: path.join(logsDir, 'combined.log'),
        maxsize: 10 * 1024 * 1024, // 10MB
        maxFiles: 10,
      }),
    ] : []),
  ],
});

// Silence in test env
if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'test') {
  logger.transports.forEach(t => { t.silent = true; });
}

module.exports = logger;

FILE: backend/src/utils/otpHelper.js
========================================================================
// src/utils/otpHelper.js
// Utilities for generating and verifying 6-digit OTPs.
//
// Security:
//   • Crypto-random generation (not Math.random — not cryptographically secure).
//   • OTPs hashed with bcrypt (cost 10) before storage.
//   • Constant-time comparison via bcrypt.compare (no timing attacks).

'use strict';

const crypto  = require('crypto');
const bcrypt  = require('bcryptjs');
const jwt     = require('jsonwebtoken');

const BCRYPT_ROUNDS = 10;
const { PREAUTH_SECRET: PRE_AUTH_SECRET } = require('../config/env');
const PRE_AUTH_TTL_SEC = 10 * 60; // 10 minutes

// ── OTP Generation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Generate a cryptographically-random 6-digit OTP string.
 * Uses rejection sampling to ensure uniform distribution.
 */
const generateOtp = () => {
  // Generate random bytes and convert to a number in [0, 999999].
  // Rejection sampling keeps distribution uniform.
  // crypto.randomInt is uniform (no modulo bias)
  const otp = crypto.randomInt(0, 1_000_000);

  // Zero-pad to 6 digits
  return otp.toString().padStart(6, '0');
};

// ── Hashing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Hash an OTP with bcrypt.
 * @param {string} otp  Plain 6-digit OTP string
 * @returns {Promise<string>}  bcrypt hash
 */
const hashOtp = (otp) => bcrypt.hash(otp, BCRYPT_ROUNDS);

/**
 * Verify a plain OTP against a bcrypt hash.
 * @param {string} otp   Plain OTP submitted by user
 * @param {string} hash  Stored bcrypt hash
 * @returns {Promise<boolean>}
 */
const verifyOtp = (otp, hash) => bcrypt.compare(otp, hash);

// ── Pre-Auth Token ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// A short-lived JWT issued after successful password check, before OTP.
// It carries ONLY { userId, email, purpose: 'otp' } and cannot be used
// to access any protected API route (checked in verifyOtp controller).

/**
 * Sign a pre-auth token.
 * @param {{ userId: string, email: string }} payload
 * @returns {string}
 */
const signPreAuthToken = ({ userId, email }) =>
  jwt.sign(
    { userId, email, purpose: 'otp' },
    PRE_AUTH_SECRET,
    { expiresIn: PRE_AUTH_TTL_SEC }
  );

/**
 * Verify and decode a pre-auth token.
 * Throws if invalid, expired, or wrong purpose.
 * @param {string} token
 * @returns {{ userId: string, email: string }}
 */
const verifyPreAuthToken = (token) => {
  const decoded = jwt.verify(token, PRE_AUTH_SECRET);
  if (decoded.purpose !== 'otp') {
    throw new Error('Invalid pre-auth token purpose');
  }
  return { userId: decoded.userId, email: decoded.email };
};

// ── Email masking helper (UI only) ────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Mask an email for display: "john.doe@gmail.com" → "j***@gmail.com"
 * @param {string} email
 * @returns {string}
 */
const maskEmail = (email) => {
  const [local, domain] = email.split('@');
  const masked = local[0] + '***';
  return `${masked}@${domain}`;
};

module.exports = {
  generateOtp,
  hashOtp,
  verifyOtp,
  signPreAuthToken,
  verifyPreAuthToken,
  maskEmail,
};

// src/utils/username.js
'use strict';

const User = require('../models/firestore/User');

/** Build a unique username from a display name / email prefix. */
async function generateUsername(displayName) {
  const base = (displayName || 'user')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')
    .slice(0, 15) || 'user';

  let candidate = base;
  let suffix = 0;
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-constant-condition
  while (true) {
    if (!(await User.findByUsername(candidate))) return candidate;
    suffix += 1;
    candidate = `${base}${suffix}`;
  }
}

module.exports = { generateUsername };

// src/utils/validation.js
'use strict';
const { validationResult } = require('express-validator');

/**
 * If the request failed validation, send a 400 that the frontend can display
 * (it reads `error`), and return true. Otherwise return false.
 */
const rejectIfInvalid = (req, res) => {
  const errors = validationResult(req);
  if (errors.isEmpty()) return false;
  const list = errors.array();
  res.status(400).json({
    success: false,
    error: list[0].msg && typeof list[0].msg === 'string' && list[0].msg !== 'Invalid value'
      ? list[0].msg
      : `Invalid value for "${list[0].path}"`,
    code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
    details: list,
  });
  return true;
};

module.exports = { rejectIfInvalid };

const nodemailer = require('nodemailer');
const dotenv = require('dotenv');

dotenv.config(); // Loads .env from current dir

const testEmail = async () => {
  console.log('Testing SMTP connection...');
  console.log('Host:', process.env.SMTP_HOST);
  console.log('Port:', process.env.SMTP_PORT);
  console.log('User:', process.env.SMTP_USER);

  const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
    host:   process.env.SMTP_HOST   || 'smtp.gmail.com',
    port:   parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT || '587'),
    secure: process.env.SMTP_SECURE === 'true',
    auth: {
      user: process.env.SMTP_USER,
      pass: process.env.SMTP_PASS ? process.env.SMTP_PASS.trim() : '',
    },
  });

  try {
    const verified = await transporter.verify();
    console.log('SMTP connection verified successfully!', verified);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('SMTP connection failed:', err.message);
  }
};

testEmail();

// tests/auth.test.js
// API tests for authentication routes (Firestore models are mocked — no real DB needed)

'use strict';

process.env.NODE_ENV = 'test';
process.env.JWT_ACCESS_SECRET = 'test_access';
process.env.JWT_REFRESH_SECRET = 'test_refresh';
process.env.SESSION_SECRET = 'test_session';

const request = require('supertest');

// The suite makes more than 10 auth calls from one IP — bypass the per-minute limiter here
jest.mock('../src/middleware/rateLimiter', () => {
  const pass = (req, res, next) => next();
  return { rateLimiter: pass, strictAuthLimiter: pass, speechRateLimiter: pass, otpResendLimiter: pass };
});

const mockVerifyIdToken = jest.fn();
jest.mock('../src/config/firebase', () => ({
  connectFirebase: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true),
  admin: { auth: () => ({ verifyIdToken: mockVerifyIdToken }) },
  db: { collection: () => ({}) },
  FieldValue: {},
  toDate: (v) => v,
}));

jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/User', () => ({
  findByEmail: jest.fn(),
  findById: jest.fn(),
  findByUsername: jest.fn(),
  findByGoogleId: jest.fn(),
  findByEmailOrUsername: jest.fn(),
  create: jest.fn(),
  update: jest.fn(),
  updateProfile: jest.fn(),
  sanitize: (u) => u,
}));
jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/UserActivity', () => ({ create: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({}) }));
jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/OtpRecord', () => ({ upsertOtp: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({}) }));
jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/AuthSession', () => ({
  createSession: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({}),
  deleteAllForUser: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({}),
}));
jest.mock('../src/Services/emailService', () => ({
  sendLoginOtp: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true),
  sendWelcome: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true),
}));

const app = require('../src/app');
const User = require('../src/models/firestore/User');
const AuthSession = require('../src/models/firestore/AuthSession');

describe('Auth API', () => {
  beforeEach(() => jest.clearAllMocks());

  describe('POST /api/v1/auth/register', () => {
    it('returns 400 for missing fields', async () => {
      const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/register').send({ email: 'invalid' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    });

    it('returns 400 for short password', async () => {
      const res = await request(app)
        .post('/api/v1/auth/register')
        .send({ email: 'test@test.com', username: 'testuser', password: '123' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    });

    it('registers and asks for OTP with valid data', async () => {
      User.findByEmailOrUsername.mockResolvedValue(null);
      User.create.mockResolvedValue({ id: 'uid-123', email: 'test@test.com', username: 'testuser', role: 'USER' });

      const res = await request(app)
        .post('/api/v1/auth/register')
        .send({ email: 'test@test.com', username: 'testuser', password: 'password123' });

      expect(res.status).toBe(201);
      expect(res.body.success).toBe(true);
      expect(res.body.requiresOtp).toBe(true);
      expect(res.body).toHaveProperty('preAuthToken');
      expect(User.create).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    it('returns 409 for duplicate user', async () => {
      User.findByEmailOrUsername.mockResolvedValue({ id: 'existing', email: 'test@test.com' });

      const res = await request(app)
        .post('/api/v1/auth/register')
        .send({ email: 'test@test.com', username: 'testuser', password: 'password123' });

      expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    });

    it('returns 409 when the transaction detects a race-condition duplicate', async () => {
      User.findByEmailOrUsername.mockResolvedValue(null);
      const err = new Error('dup'); err.code = 'DUPLICATE_USERNAME';
      User.create.mockRejectedValue(err);

      const res = await request(app)
        .post('/api/v1/auth/register')
        .send({ email: 'new@test.com', username: 'taken', password: 'password123' });

      expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    });
  });

  describe('POST /api/v1/auth/login', () => {
    it('returns 400 for missing credentials', async () => {
      const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/login').send({});
      expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    });

    it('returns 401 for non-existent user', async () => {
      User.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue(null);
      const res = await request(app)
        .post('/api/v1/auth/login')
        .send({ email: 'noone@test.com', password: 'password' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    });
  });

  describe('POST /api/v1/auth/google (Firebase Auth)', () => {
    const goodToken = (over = {}) => ({
      uid: 'fb-uid-1', email: 'g@test.com', email_verified: true, name: 'Gee User', picture: 'http://img/p.png',
      firebase: { sign_in_provider: 'google.com' }, ...over,
    });

    it('returns 400 when idToken is missing', async () => {
      const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/google').send({});
      expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    });

    it('returns 401 when the Firebase token is invalid', async () => {
      mockVerifyIdToken.mockRejectedValue(new Error('bad token'));
      const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/google').send({ idToken: 'x' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(401);
      expect(res.body.code).toBe('INVALID_ID_TOKEN');
    });

    it('rejects non-Google providers', async () => {
      mockVerifyIdToken.mockResolvedValue(goodToken({ firebase: { sign_in_provider: 'password' } }));
      const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/google').send({ idToken: 'x' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    });

    it('rejects unverified Google emails', async () => {
      mockVerifyIdToken.mockResolvedValue(goodToken({ email_verified: false }));
      const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/google').send({ idToken: 'x' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    });

    it('creates a new user and returns a session', async () => {
      mockVerifyIdToken.mockResolvedValue(goodToken());
      User.findByGoogleId.mockResolvedValue(null);
      User.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue(null);
      User.findByUsername.mockResolvedValue(null);
      User.create.mockResolvedValue({ id: 'u1', email: 'g@test.com', username: 'geeuser', role: 'USER', emailVerified: true });
      User.updateProfile.mockResolvedValue({});

      const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/google').send({ idToken: 'good' });

      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      expect(res.body.data).toHaveProperty('accessToken');
      expect(res.body.data.user.email).toBe('g@test.com');
      expect(User.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ googleId: 'fb-uid-1', email: 'g@test.com' }));
      expect(res.headers['set-cookie'].join(';')).toMatch(/accessToken=/);
    });

    it('links an existing verified account without touching its password', async () => {
      mockVerifyIdToken.mockResolvedValue(goodToken());
      User.findByGoogleId.mockResolvedValue(null);
      User.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue({ id: 'u2', email: 'g@test.com', username: 'old', role: 'USER', emailVerified: true, passwordHash: 'hash', avatar: null });
      User.update.mockImplementation(async (id, patch) => ({ id, email: 'g@test.com', username: 'old', role: 'USER', ...patch }));

      const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/google').send({ idToken: 'good' });

      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      const patch = User.update.mock.calls[0][1];
      expect(patch.googleId).toBe('fb-uid-1');
      expect(patch).not.toHaveProperty('passwordHash');
    });

    it('wipes the password when linking an UNVERIFIED password account (pre-hijack defence)', async () => {
      mockVerifyIdToken.mockResolvedValue(goodToken());
      User.findByGoogleId.mockResolvedValue(null);
      User.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue({ id: 'u3', email: 'g@test.com', username: 'squat', role: 'USER', emailVerified: false, passwordHash: 'attackerhash' });
      User.update.mockImplementation(async (id, patch) => ({ id, email: 'g@test.com', username: 'squat', role: 'USER', ...patch }));

      const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/google').send({ idToken: 'good' });

      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      const patch = User.update.mock.calls[0][1];
      expect(patch.passwordHash).toBe('');
      expect(patch.emailVerified).toBe(true);
      expect(AuthSession.deleteAllForUser).toHaveBeenCalledWith('u3');
    });
  });

  describe('GET /health', () => {
    it('returns 200', async () => {
      const res = await request(app).get('/health');
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      expect(res.body.status).toBe('ok');
    });
  });

  describe('GET /api/v1/unknown', () => {
    it('returns 404 for unknown routes', async () => {
      const res = await request(app).get('/api/v1/unknown-route');
      expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    });
  });
});

// tests/integration.test.js
// Walks through the exact calls the Next.js frontend makes (lib/api.ts), against the real Express app +
// Socket.IO, with in-memory stand-ins for the Firestore model layer.
'use strict';

process.env.NODE_ENV = 'test';
process.env.JWT_ACCESS_SECRET = 'test_access';
process.env.JWT_REFRESH_SECRET = 'test_refresh';
process.env.SESSION_SECRET = 'test_session';
delete process.env.GROQ_API_KEY;   // must boot (and fall back to mock analysis) without it
delete process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGINS; // socket must still accept localhost in dev

jest.mock('../src/middleware/rateLimiter', () => {
  const pass = (req, res, next) => next();
  return { rateLimiter: pass, strictAuthLimiter: pass, speechRateLimiter: pass, otpResendLimiter: pass };
});
jest.mock('../src/config/firebase', () => ({
  connectFirebase: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true),
  admin: { auth: () => ({ verifyIdToken: jest.fn() }) },
  db: { collection: () => ({}) }, FieldValue: {}, toDate: (v) => v,
}));

// ── in-memory model layer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const mockStore = { users: new Map(), otp: new Map(), sessions: new Map(), practice: [], chat: [], pron: [], activity: new Map(), seq: 0 };
const mockId = () => `id${++mockStore.seq}`;

jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/User', () => {
  const U = {
    findById: async (i) => mockStore.users.get(i) || null,
    findByEmail: async (e) => [...mockStore.users.values()].find((u) => u.email === e) || null,
    findByUsername: async (n) => [...mockStore.users.values()].find((u) => u.username === n) || null,
    findByGoogleId: async () => null,
    findByEmailOrUsername: async (e, n) => (await U.findByEmail(e)) || (await U.findByUsername(n)),
    create: async ({ email, username, passwordHash = '', role = 'USER' }) => {
      const u = { id: mockId(), email, username, passwordHash, role, emailVerified: false, createdAt: new Date(), profile: {} };
      mockStore.users.set(u.id, u); return u;
    },
    update: async (i, d) => { Object.assign(mockStore.users.get(i), d); return mockStore.users.get(i); },
    updateProfile: async (i, d) => { Object.assign(mockStore.users.get(i).profile, d); return mockStore.users.get(i).profile; },
    findManyByIds: async (ids) => Object.fromEntries(ids.filter((i) => mockStore.users.has(i)).map((i) => [i, mockStore.users.get(i)])),
    sanitize: ({ passwordHash, googleId, ...r }) => r,
  };
  return U;
});
jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/OtpRecord', () => ({
  upsertOtp: async ({ userId, email, otpHash }) => {
    const ex = mockStore.otp.get(userId); const now = new Date();
    const blocked = ex?.blockUntil && ex.blockUntil > now;
    mockStore.otp.set(userId, { userId, email, otpHash, expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 300000),
      attempts: blocked ? ex.attempts : 0, blockUntil: blocked ? ex.blockUntil : null,
      resendCount: (ex?.resendCount || 0) + 1, resendWindowStart: ex?.resendWindowStart || now });
  },
  findOne: async ({ userId }) => mockStore.otp.get(userId) || null,
  findActive: async (u) => { const r = mockStore.otp.get(u); return r && r.expiresAt > new Date() ? r : null; },
  recordFailedAttempt: async (u) => { const r = mockStore.otp.get(u); r.attempts++; if (r.attempts >= 5) r.blockUntil = new Date(Date.now() + 900000); return { ...r }; },
  invalidate: async (u) => { mockStore.otp.delete(u); },
}));
jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/AuthSession', () => ({
  createSession: async ({ tokenHash, userId }) => { mockStore.sessions.set(tokenHash, { userId, tokenHash, prev: null, expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 1e9), lastUsedAt: new Date() }); },
  findByToken: async (h) => {
    const c = mockStore.sessions.get(h); if (c) return c;
    return [...mockStore.sessions.values()].find((s) => s.prev === h) || null;
  },
  rotateToken: async (oldH, newH) => {
    const o = mockStore.sessions.get(oldH); if (!o) return null;
    mockStore.sessions.delete(oldH); mockStore.sessions.set(newH, { ...o, tokenHash: newH, prev: oldH, lastUsedAt: new Date() }); return true;
  },
  deleteByToken: async (h) => { mockStore.sessions.delete(h); },
  deleteAllForUser: async (u) => { for (const [k, v] of mockStore.sessions) if (v.userId === u) mockStore.sessions.delete(k); },
  getActiveSessions: async () => [],
}));
jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/UserActivity', () => ({
  ensure: async (u) => { if (!mockStore.activity.has(u)) mockStore.activity.set(u, { userId: u, totalSessions: 0, totalMinutes: 0, streak: { current: 0, longest: 0 } }); },
  create: async ({ userId }) => { mockStore.activity.set(userId, { userId, totalSessions: 0, totalMinutes: 0, streak: { current: 0, longest: 0 } }); },
  findByUserId: async (u) => mockStore.activity.get(u) || null,
  addSession: async (u) => { const a = mockStore.activity.get(u); a.totalSessions++; a.streak.current = 1; },
  getLeaderboard: async () => [...mockStore.activity.values()],
}));
jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/PracticeSession', () => ({
  create: async (d) => { const s = { id: mockId(), ...d, createdAt: new Date() }; mockStore.practice.push(s); return s; },
  statsFor: async (u) => mockStore.practice.filter((s) => s.userId === u),
  paginate: async (u, { language } = {}) => { const l = mockStore.practice.filter((s) => s.userId === u && (!language || s.language === language)); return { sessions: l, total: l.length }; },
  findOwned: async (i, u) => mockStore.practice.find((s) => s.id === i && s.userId === u) || null,
  delete: async () => {}, count: async () => mockStore.practice.length,
}));
jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/PronunciationRecord', () => ({
  create: async (d) => { const r = { _id: mockId(), id: mockId(), ...d }; mockStore.pron.push(r); return r; },
  paginate: async () => ({ records: mockStore.pron, total: mockStore.pron.length }),
  getAccuracyTrend: async () => [{ _id: '2026-01-01', avgAccuracy: 80, count: 1 }],
}));
jest.mock('../src/models/firestore/ChatMessage', () => ({
  create: async ({ userId, username, content, roomId, type }) => {
    const m = { id: mockId(), userId, roomId, content, type, createdAt: new Date(), user: { id: userId, username } };
    mockStore.chat.push(m); return m;
  },
  paginate: async (roomId) => ({ messages: mockStore.chat.filter((m) => m.roomId === roomId), total: mockStore.chat.length }),
}));

let mockLastOtp = null;
jest.mock('../src/Services/emailService', () => ({
  sendLoginOtp: jest.fn(async (to, { otp }) => { mockLastOtp = otp; return true; }),
  sendWelcome: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true),
  sendPasswordReset: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true),
  sendPasswordChanged: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(true),
}));

const http = require('http');
const request = require('supertest');
const { io: ioClient } = require('socket.io-client');
const app = require('../src/app');
const { initSocket } = require('../src/sockets/socketManager');

const EMAIL = 'john.doe@gmail.com';
let server, ioServer, port, token, refreshCookie;

beforeAll((done) => {
  server = http.createServer(app);
  ioServer = initSocket(server);
  app.set('io', ioServer);
  server.listen(0, () => { port = server.address().port; done(); });
});
afterAll((done) => { ioServer.close(); server.close(done); });

const auth = (r) => r.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);

describe('frontend ⇄ backend contract', () => {
  test('register keeps the typed email (no Gmail dot-stripping) and asks for OTP', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/register').send({ email: EMAIL, username: 'john_doe', password: 'password123' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    expect(res.body.requiresOtp).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.preAuthToken).toBeTruthy();
    expect([...mockStore.users.values()][0].email).toBe(EMAIL);
    token = null; global.pre = res.body.preAuthToken;
  });

  test('validation errors carry a readable `error` string', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/register').send({ email: EMAIL, username: 'a', password: 'password123' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(typeof res.body.error).toBe('string');
    expect(res.body.error).toMatch(/Username/);
    const login = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/login').send({ email: EMAIL });
    expect(login.status).toBe(400);
    expect(login.body.error).toMatch(/Password/);
  });

  test('wrong OTP -> 401 with code, right OTP -> accessToken in the body + cookies', async () => {
    const bad = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/verify-otp').send({ preAuthToken: global.pre, otp: '000000' === mockLastOtp ? '111111' : '000000' });
    expect(bad.status).toBe(401);
    expect(bad.body.code).toBe('OTP_INVALID');

    const ok = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/verify-otp').send({ preAuthToken: global.pre, otp: mockLastOtp });
    expect(ok.status).toBe(200);
    expect(ok.body.data.accessToken).toBeTruthy();
    expect(ok.body.data.user.passwordHash).toBeUndefined();
    token = ok.body.data.accessToken;
    refreshCookie = ok.headers['set-cookie'].find((c) => c.startsWith('refreshToken=')).split(';')[0];
  });

  test('pre-auth token can NOT be used as an access token', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/v1/users/me').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${global.pre}`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  test('GET /users/me nests activity inside user (dashboard streak)', async () => {
    const res = await auth(request(app).get('/api/v1/users/me'));
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.user.activity.streak.current).toBe(0);
    expect(res.body.data.user.googleId).toBeUndefined();
  });

  test('verified user login returns accessToken in the body', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/login').send({ email: EMAIL, password: 'password123' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.accessToken).toBeTruthy();
  });

  test('refresh: cookie -> new access token; replay of the old cookie within grace still works; bad token -> 401 (not 500)', async () => {
    const r1 = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/refresh').set('Cookie', refreshCookie);
    expect(r1.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r1.body.data.accessToken).toBeTruthy();
    const bad = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/refresh').set('Cookie', 'refreshToken=garbage');
    expect(bad.status).toBe(401);
    const none = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/refresh');
    expect(none.status).toBe(401);
  });

  test('speech/analyze works without GROQ_API_KEY and returns the fields the Practice page reads', async () => {
    const res = await auth(request(app).post('/api/v1/speech/analyze')).send({ targetText: 'Hello how are you', transcribedText: 'hello how are you', language: 'en', sessionId: 's1' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const d = res.body.data;
    expect(typeof d.overallAccuracy).toBe('number');
    expect(Array.isArray(d.wordsBreakdown)).toBe(true);
    expect(Array.isArray(d.suggestions)).toBe(true);
    const empty = await auth(request(app).post('/api/v1/speech/analyze')).send({ targetText: 'Hi', transcribedText: '' });
    expect(empty.status).toBe(400);
    expect(empty.body.error).toMatch(/transcribed/i);
  });

  test('sessions: create with exactly what the Practice page sends, then list for the Sessions page', async () => {
    const c = await auth(request(app).post('/api/v1/sessions')).send({
      language: 'en', duration: 12, accuracy: 87.5, wordsAttempted: 4, wordsCorrect: 3, transcript: 'hello how are you', feedback: 'a; b',
    });
    expect(c.status).toBe(201);
    const l = await auth(request(app).get('/api/v1/sessions?page=1&limit=10&language=en'));
    expect(l.status).toBe(200);
    expect(l.body.data.sessions).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(l.body.data.pagination.totalPages).toBe(1);
    const bad = await auth(request(app).post('/api/v1/sessions')).send({ language: 'en', duration: 5, accuracy: 140, wordsAttempted: 1, wordsCorrect: 1 });
    expect(bad.status).toBe(400);
    expect(bad.body.error).toMatch(/Accuracy/);
  });

  test('accuracy trend + leaderboard rows include username', async () => {
    const t = await auth(request(app).get('/api/v1/speech/accuracy-trend?days=30'));
    expect(t.body.data.trend[0]).toMatchObject({ _id: expect.any(String), avgAccuracy: expect.any(Number) });
    const lb = await auth(request(app).get('/api/v1/users/leaderboard'));
    expect(lb.body.data.leaderboard[0].username).toBe('john_doe');
  });

  test('tutor: demo fallback keeps {reply, translation, score} shape; scenario prompt is accepted', async () => {
    const res = await auth(request(app).post('/api/v1/conversation/chat')).send({
      userMessage: 'Bonjour', language: 'French', conversationHistory: [{ role: 'ai', text: 'Salut' }], systemPrompt: 'SCENARIO: travel',
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.reply).toBeTruthy();
    expect(typeof res.body.data.score).toBe('number');
  });

  test('phrases: unknown lang/difficulty are coerced, not cached or sent to the LLM', async () => {
    const res = await auth(request(app).get('/api/v1/phrases/daily?lang=zz&difficulty=evil'));
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.languageCode).toBe('en');
    expect(res.body.data.difficulty).toBe('intermediate');
    expect(res.body.data.phrases.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  test('CORS preflight from localhost:3000 succeeds with credentials; unknown origin gets no CORS header (and no 500)', async () => {
    const ok = await request(app).options('/api/v1/users/me').set('Origin', 'http://localhost:3000')
      .set('Access-Control-Request-Method', 'GET').set('Access-Control-Request-Headers', 'authorization');
    expect(ok.status).toBe(204);
    expect(ok.headers['access-control-allow-credentials']).toBe('true');
    const bad = await request(app).get('/health').set('Origin', 'https://evil.example');
    expect(bad.status).toBe(200);
    expect(bad.headers['access-control-allow-origin']).toBeUndefined();
  });

  test('OTP lockout cannot be bypassed by requesting a new code', async () => {
    const reg = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/register').send({ email: 'lock@test.com', username: 'locky', password: 'password123' });
    const pre = reg.body.preAuthToken;
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/verify-otp').send({ preAuthToken: pre, otp: mockLastOtp === '123456' ? '654321' : '123456' });
    const resend = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/resend-otp').send({ preAuthToken: pre });
    expect(resend.status).toBe(429);
    expect(resend.body.code).toBe('OTP_BLOCKED');
    const tryRight = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/verify-otp').send({ preAuthToken: pre, otp: mockLastOtp });
    expect(tryRight.status).toBe(429);
  });

  test('forgot/reset password: link points at the Next route; weak password rejected by the server', async () => {
    const weak = await request(app).post('/api/v1/auth/reset-password/sometoken').send({ password: '123' });
    expect(weak.status).toBe(400);
    expect(weak.body.error).toMatch(/8 chars/);
  });
});

describe('Socket.IO (community page)', () => {
  const connect = (t) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const s = ioClient(`http://localhost:${port}`, { auth: { token: t }, transports: ['websocket'], extraHeaders: { Origin: 'http://localhost:3000' } });
    s.on('connect', () => resolve(s));
    s.on('connect_error', (e) => { s.close(); reject(e); });
  });

  test('rejects a missing/invalid token', async () => {
    await expect(connect(null)).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(connect('nope')).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  test('two clients in the same room exchange a message with the shape toMsg() reads', async () => {
    const a = await connect(token);
    const b = await connect(token);
    a.emit('chat:join', 'global'); b.emit('chat:join', 'global');
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
    const got = new Promise((res) => b.on('chat:message', res));
    a.emit('chat:message', { content: 'hello room', roomId: 'global' });
    const m = await got;
    expect(m).toMatchObject({ content: 'hello room', roomId: 'global', user: { username: 'john_doe' } });
    expect(m.createdAt).toBeTruthy();
    // history endpoint returns the persisted message
    const h = await auth(request(app).get('/api/v1/chat/messages?roomId=global&page=1'));
    expect(h.body.data.messages.some((x) => x.content === 'hello room')).toBe(true);
    a.close(); b.close();
  });
});

FILE: frontend-react/README.md
========================================================================
# LinguaWave — React frontend (Vite + JavaScript)

Pixel-for-pixel port of the Next.js (`frontend/`) app to plain React + Vite.
All CSS files are copied unchanged; the backend is untouched and every API / Socket.IO call is identical.

## Run

```bash
cp .env.example .env      # set VITE_API_URL etc.
npm install
npm run dev               # http://localhost:3000
npm run build             # outputs dist/
```

## Routes (React Router)

| Path | Page |
|------|------|
| `/` | Landing + auth modal (`?error=oauth_failed`, `?verified=1` handled) |
| `/reset-password?token=…` | Reset password |
| `/dashboard` | Practice |
| `/dashboard/tutor` · `progress` · `community` · `sessions` · `leaderboard` | Dashboard pages |

## Changes from the Next.js version

- `next/link` → `react-router-dom` `<Link to>`; `useRouter/usePathname/useSearchParams` → router hooks
- `next/font` → Google Fonts `<link>` in `index.html` + `src/styles/fonts.css` (same `--font-*` variables)
- `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` → `VITE_*` env vars
- Dashboard `layout.tsx` → `DashboardLayout.jsx` using `<Outlet />`

## Backend CORS

Add your frontend origin (default `http://localhost:3000`) to `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` / `FRONTEND_URL` in the backend `.env`.

## Deploy (Render static site)

Build command `npm install && npm run build`, publish dir `dist`, and add a rewrite `/*` → `/index.html`
(`public/_redirects` and `vercel.json` are included for Netlify/Vercel).

      rel="stylesheet"

FILE: frontend-react/src/App.jsx

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { Navigate, Route, Routes, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import Landing from '@/pages/Landing';
import ResetPassword from '@/pages/ResetPassword';
import DashboardLayout from '@/pages/dashboard/DashboardLayout';
import Practice from '@/pages/dashboard/Practice';
import Tutor from '@/pages/dashboard/Tutor';
import Progress from '@/pages/dashboard/Progress';
import Community from '@/pages/dashboard/Community';
import Sessions from '@/pages/dashboard/Sessions';
import Leaderboard from '@/pages/dashboard/Leaderboard';

function ScrollToTop() {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  useEffect(() => { window.scrollTo(0, 0); }, [pathname]);
  return null;
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <>
      <ScrollToTop />
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/" element={<Landing />} />
        <Route path="/reset-password" element={<ResetPassword />} />
        <Route path="/dashboard" element={<DashboardLayout />}>
          <Route index element={<Practice />} />
          <Route path="tutor" element={<Tutor />} />
          <Route path="progress" element={<Progress />} />
          <Route path="community" element={<Community />} />
          <Route path="sessions" element={<Sessions />} />
          <Route path="leaderboard" element={<Leaderboard />} />
        </Route>
        <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
      </Routes>
    </>
  );
}

========================================================================
FILE: frontend-react/src/components/AuthModal.jsx
========================================================================
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { getGoogleIdToken } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
const emailOk = (v) => /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(v);
function strength(pw) {
    let s = 0;
    if (pw.length >= 8)
        s++;
    if (pw.length >= 12)
        s++;
    if (/[A-Z]/.test(pw))
        s++;
    if (/[0-9]/.test(pw))
        s++;
    if (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(pw))
        s++;
    if (s <= 1)
        return { pct: 25, color: '#FB7185', label: 'Weak' };
    if (s <= 2)
        return { pct: 50, color: '#FBBF24', label: 'Fair' };
    if (s <= 3)
        return { pct: 75, color: '#4ADE80', label: 'Good' };
    return { pct: 100, color: '#2DD4BF', label: 'Strong' };
}
export default function AuthModal({ initialTab, onClose }) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { reload } = useAuth();
    const [tab, setTab] = useState(initialTab);
    const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
    const [msg, setMsg] = useState(null);
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [showPw, setShowPw] = useState(false);
    const [preAuth, setPreAuth] = useState(null);
    const [masked, setMasked] = useState('');
    const [digits, setDigits] = useState(Array(6).fill(''));
    const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState(0);
    const otpRefs = useRef([]);
    useEffect(() => {
        const onKey = (e) => e.key === 'Escape' && onClose();
        document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
        return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    }, [onClose]);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (countdown <= 0)
            return;
        const t = setTimeout(() => setCountdown((c) => c - 1), 1000);
        return () => clearTimeout(t);
    }, [countdown]);
    const switchTab = (t) => { setTab(t); setMsg(null); setErrors({}); };
    const enterOtp = (token, maskedEmail) => {
        setPreAuth(token);
        setMasked(maskedEmail);
        setDigits(Array(6).fill(''));
        setCountdown(60);
        setTab('otp');
        setMsg(null);
        setTimeout(() => otpRefs.current[0]?.focus(), 100);
    };
    const finishLogin = async () => {
        await reload();
        navigate('/dashboard');
    };
    const continueWithGoogle = async () => {
        setBusy(true);
        setMsg(null);
        try {
            const idToken = await getGoogleIdToken();
            await api.auth.google(idToken);
            await finishLogin();
        }
        catch (err) {
            // user closed the popup / opened another one — not an error worth showing
            if (err?.code === 'auth/popup-closed-by-user' || err?.code === 'auth/cancelled-popup-request')
                return;
            const text = err?.code === 'auth/popup-blocked'
                ? 'Your browser blocked the Google popup. Allow popups and try again.'
                : err?.message || 'Google sign-in failed. Please try again.';
            setMsg({ type: 'error', text });
        }
        finally {
            setBusy(false);
        }
    };
    const submitLogin = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const errs = {};
        if (!emailOk(email))
            errs.email = 'Enter a valid email';
        if (!password)
            errs.password = 'Password is required';
        setErrors(errs);
        if (Object.keys(errs).length)
            return;
        setBusy(true);
        setMsg(null);
        try {
            const res = await api.auth.login({ email, password });
            if (res.requiresOtp)
                return enterOtp(res.preAuthToken, res.maskedEmail || email);
            await finishLogin();
        }
        catch (err) {
            setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Invalid credentials' });
        }
        finally {
            setBusy(false);
        }
    };
    const submitRegister = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const errs = {};
        if (!emailOk(email))
            errs.email = 'Enter a valid email';
        if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{3,20}$/.test(username))
            errs.username = '3–20 letters, numbers or underscores';
        if (password.length < 8)
            errs.password = 'At least 8 characters';
        setErrors(errs);
        if (Object.keys(errs).length)
            return;
        setBusy(true);
        setMsg(null);
        try {
            const res = await api.auth.register({ email, username, password });
            if (res.requiresOtp)
                return enterOtp(res.preAuthToken, res.maskedEmail || email);
            await finishLogin();
        }
        catch (err) {
            setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Registration failed' });
        }
        finally {
            setBusy(false);
        }
    };
    const submitOtp = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const otp = digits.join('');
        if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(otp))
            return setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Enter all 6 digits.' });
        if (!preAuth) {
            setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Session expired. Please log in again.' });
            return switchTab('login');
        }
        setBusy(true);
        setMsg(null);
        try {
            await api.auth.verifyOtp({ preAuthToken: preAuth, otp });
            await finishLogin();
        }
        catch (err) {
            setDigits(Array(6).fill(''));
            otpRefs.current[0]?.focus();
            if (err.code === 'OTP_EXPIRED' || err.code === 'PRE_AUTH_EXPIRED') {
                setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Your code expired. Please log in again.' });
                setTimeout(() => switchTab('login'), 1800);
            }
            else
                setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Incorrect code.' });
        }
        finally {
            setBusy(false);
        }
    };
    const resend = async () => {
        if (!preAuth || countdown > 0)
            return;
        try {
            await api.auth.resendOtp({ preAuthToken: preAuth });
            setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'New code sent. Check your email.' });
            setCountdown(60);
        }
        catch (err) {
            setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Could not resend code.' });
        }
    };
    const onDigit = (i, v) => {
        const d = v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-1);
        setDigits((s) => s.map((x, idx) => (idx === i ? d : x)));
        if (d && i < 5)
            otpRefs.current[i + 1]?.focus();
    };
    const onPaste = (e) => {
        const pasted = e.clipboardData.getData('text').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6);
        if (!pasted)
            return;
        e.preventDefault();
        setDigits(Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => pasted[i] || ''));
        otpRefs.current[Math.min(pasted.length, 5)]?.focus();
    };
    const st = strength(password);
    return (<div className="overlay" onMouseDown={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()} role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
      <div className="modal">
        <button className="modal-close" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">×</button>

        {tab !== 'otp' && (<div className="tabs">
            <button className={`tab ${tab === 'login' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => switchTab('login')}>Log in</button>
            <button className={`tab ${tab === 'register' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => switchTab('register')}>Sign up</button>
          </div>)}

        {msg && <div className={`form-msg ${msg.type}`}>{msg.text}</div>}

        {tab === 'login' && (<form onSubmit={submitLogin} noValidate>
            <h2>Welcome back</h2>
            <p className="muted small" style={{ marginBottom: 18 }}>Continue your language journey.</p>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="le">Email</label>
              <input id="le" className="input" type="email" autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com"/>
              <span className="field-error">{errors.email}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="lp">Password</label>
              <div className="pw-wrap">
                <input id="lp" className="input" type={showPw ? 'text' : 'password'} autoComplete="current-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}/>
                <button type="button" className="pw-toggle" onClick={() => setShowPw((s) => !s)}>{showPw ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}</button>
              </div>
              <span className="field-error">{errors.password}</span>
            </div>
            <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner"/> : 'Log in'}</button>
            <div className="divider">or</div>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-outline btn-block" disabled={busy} onClick={continueWithGoogle}>Continue with Google</button>
            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 14 }} className="small">
              <Link to="/reset-password" className="muted">Forgot password?</Link>
            </p>
          </form>)}

        {tab === 'register' && (<form onSubmit={submitRegister} noValidate>
            <h2>Create your account</h2>
            <p className="muted small" style={{ marginBottom: 18 }}>Start speaking better today.</p>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="re">Email</label>
              <input id="re" className="input" type="email" autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com"/>
              <span className="field-error">{errors.email}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="ru">Username</label>
              <input id="ru" className="input" value={username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} placeholder="linguastar" maxLength={20}/>
              <span className="field-error">{errors.username}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="rp">Password</label>
              <div className="pw-wrap">
                <input id="rp" className="input" type={showPw ? 'text' : 'password'} autoComplete="new-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="Min. 8 characters"/>
                <button type="button" className="pw-toggle" onClick={() => setShowPw((s) => !s)}>{showPw ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}</button>
              </div>
              {password && <div className="strength"><div style={{ width: `${st.pct}%`, background: st.color }}/></div>}
              <span className="field-error">{errors.password}</span>
            </div>
            <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner"/> : 'Create account'}</button>
          </form>)}

        {tab === 'otp' && (<form onSubmit={submitOtp} noValidate>
            <button type="button" className="btn-ghost small" onClick={() => switchTab('login')}>← Back</button>
            <h2 style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>Verify your email</h2>
            <p className="muted small">We sent a 6-digit code to <b>{masked}</b>. It expires in 5 minutes.</p>
            <div className="otp-row" onPaste={onPaste}>
              {digits.map((d, i) => (<input key={i} ref={(el) => { otpRefs.current[i] = el; }} inputMode="numeric" maxLength={1} value={d} aria-label={`Digit ${i + 1}`} onChange={(e) => onDigit(i, e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Backspace' && !digits[i] && i > 0 && otpRefs.current[i - 1]?.focus()}/>))}
            </div>
            <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner"/> : 'Verify & continue'}</button>
            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 14 }} className="small muted">
              Didn’t get it?{' '}
              <button type="button" onClick={resend} disabled={countdown > 0} style={{ color: countdown > 0 ? undefined : 'var(--accent)', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                Resend{countdown > 0 ? ` (${countdown}s)` : ''}
              </button>
            </p>
          </form>)}
      </div>
    </div>);
}

import { BarElement, CategoryScale, Chart as ChartJS, Filler, LinearScale, LineElement, PointElement, Tooltip, Legend, } from 'chart.js';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Bar, Line } from 'react-chartjs-2';
ChartJS.register(CategoryScale, LinearScale, PointElement, LineElement, BarElement, Filler, Tooltip, Legend);
const TEAL = '#14B8A6';
const MUTED = '#8B98A5';
const GRID = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
const tooltip = {
    backgroundColor: '#111A22', borderColor: '#1E2A35', borderWidth: 1, titleColor: '#E6EDF3',
    bodyColor: '#8B98A5', padding: 10, cornerRadius: 8, displayColors: false,
};
/** Canvas can't read CSS variables, so resolve the real (web font) family from the DOM before drawing. */
function useChartBox(height) {
    const ref = useRef(null);
    const [font, setFont] = useState(null);
    const [reduce, setReduce] = useState(false);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (ref.current)
            setFont(getComputedStyle(ref.current).fontFamily);
        setReduce(window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);
    }, []);
    const box = (children) => (<div className="chart-box" ref={ref} style={{ height }}>{font ? children : null}</div>);
    return { box, font: font || 'system-ui', reduce };
}
// Chart.js "progressive line" so the line draws in left to right.
function drawIn(n) {
    const total = 900;
    const step = total / Math.max(1, n);
    const prevY = (ctx) => ctx.index === 0
        ? ctx.chart.scales.y.getPixelForValue(ctx.chart.scales.y.min)
        : ctx.chart.getDatasetMeta(ctx.datasetIndex).data[ctx.index - 1].getProps(['y'], true).y;
    return {
        x: {
            type: 'number', easing: 'linear', duration: step, from: NaN,
            delay(ctx) { if (ctx.type !== 'data' || ctx.xStarted)
                return 0; ctx.xStarted = true; return ctx.index * step; },
        },
        y: {
            type: 'number', easing: 'linear', duration: step, from: prevY,
            delay(ctx) { if (ctx.type !== 'data' || ctx.yStarted)
                return 0; ctx.yStarted = true; return ctx.index * step; },
        },
    };
}
export function AccuracyLine({ labels, data, label = 'Accuracy', height = 260, zoom = false, highlightLast = false, }) {
    const { box, font, reduce } = useChartBox(height);
    const min = zoom && data.length ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((Math.min(...data) - 5) / 10) * 10) : 0;
    const lastIdx = data.length - 1;
    const isLast = (i) => highlightLast && i === lastIdx;
    return box(<Line data={{
            labels,
            datasets: [{
                    label, data, borderColor: TEAL, backgroundColor: 'rgba(20,184,166,0.10)',
                    pointBackgroundColor: highlightLast ? data.map((_, i) => (isLast(i) ? '#FFFFFF' : TEAL)) : TEAL,
                    pointBorderColor: TEAL,
                    pointBorderWidth: highlightLast ? data.map((_, i) => (isLast(i) ? 2.5 : 1)) : 1,
                    fill: true, tension: 0.35,
                    pointRadius: highlightLast ? data.map((_, i) => (isLast(i) ? 5 : 3)) : data.length > 30 ? 0 : 3,
                    pointHoverRadius: 6, borderWidth: 2,
                }],
        }} options={{
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
            animation: reduce ? false : drawIn(data.length),
            font: { family: font },
            color: MUTED,
            plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: { ...tooltip, callbacks: { label: (c) => ` ${c.parsed.y}%` } } },
            scales: {
                x: { grid: { display: false }, border: { display: false }, ticks: { color: MUTED, maxTicksLimit: 7 } },
                y: { min, max: 100, grid: { color: GRID }, border: { display: false }, ticks: { color: MUTED, callback: (v) => `${v}%` } },
            },
        }}/>);
}
export function CountBars({ labels, data, label = 'Sessions', height = 260, }) {
    const { box, font, reduce } = useChartBox(height);
    return box(<Bar data={{ labels, datasets: [{ label, data, backgroundColor: TEAL, hoverBackgroundColor: '#19C6B3', borderRadius: 4, maxBarThickness: 28 }] }} options={{
            responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
            animation: reduce ? false : { duration: 700, easing: 'easeOutQuart' },
            font: { family: font },
            color: MUTED,
            plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip },
            scales: {
                x: { grid: { display: false }, border: { display: false }, ticks: { color: MUTED, maxTicksLimit: 7 } },
                y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { color: GRID }, border: { display: false }, ticks: { color: MUTED, precision: 0 } },
            },
        }}/>);
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import Icon from './Icon';
const cx = (...a) => a.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
/* ── PageHeader ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function PageHeader({ title, subtitle, actions }) {
    return (<div className="page-header">
      <div className="ph-text">
        <h1>{title}</h1>
        {subtitle && <p>{subtitle}</p>}
      </div>
      {actions && <div className="ph-actions">{actions}</div>}
    </div>);
}
/* ── Card / CardTitle ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function Card({ children, className, style, padded = true, as: Tag = 'section', ...rest }) {
    const T = Tag;
    return <T className={cx('ui-card', !padded && 'flush', className)} style={style} {...rest}>{children}</T>;
}
export function CardTitle({ icon, children, action }) {
    return (<div className="card-title">
      <h2>{icon && <Icon name={icon} size={20}/>}{children}</h2>
      {action}
    </div>);
}
/* ── SegmentedControl (sliding pill) ────────────────────────────────────── */
export function SegmentedControl({ options, value, onChange, ariaLabel, }) {
    const idx = Math.max(0, options.findIndex((o) => o.value === value));
    return (<div className="segmented" role="radiogroup" aria-label={ariaLabel} style={{ '--n': options.length, '--idx': idx }}>
      <span className="seg-pill" aria-hidden="true"/>
      {options.map((o) => (<button key={String(o.value)} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={o.value === value} className={o.value === value ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => onChange(o.value)}>
          {o.label}
        </button>))}
    </div>);
}

      {dot && <i className="chip-dot"/>}
      {icon && <Icon name={icon} size={14}/>}

    </span>);
}
/** Map a 0-100 score to a chip tone. */
export const scoreTone = (n) => (n >= 80 ? 'green' : n >= 60 ? 'amber' : 'red');
/* ── EmptyState ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function EmptyState({ icon, title, message, action }) {
    return (<div className="empty-state">

      {title && <strong>{title}</strong>}

    </div>);

/* ── Skeleton ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function Skeleton({ width = '100%', height = 16, radius = 8, className }) {
    return <span className={cx('skeleton', className)} style={{ width, height, borderRadius: radius }} aria-hidden="true"/>;
}
/* ── Count-up (once, on first value) ────────────────────────────────────── */
export function useCountUp(target, duration = 700) {
    const [val, setVal] = useState(0);
    const done = useRef(false);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (target == null)
            return;
        const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
        if (done.current || reduce) {
            setVal(target);
            done.current = true;
            return;
        }
        done.current = true;
        const start = performance.now();
        let raf = 0;
        const tick = (now) => {
            const t = Math.min(1, (now - start) / duration);
            const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
            setVal(target * eased);
            if (t < 1)
                raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
            else
                setVal(target);
        };
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    }, [target, duration]);
    return val;
}
export function CountUp({ value, decimals = 0, suffix = '' }) {
    const v = useCountUp(value);
    if (value == null)
        return <>—</>;
    return <>{v.toFixed(decimals)}{suffix}</>;
}
/* ── StatCard ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function StatCard({ label, value, decimals = 0, suffix, hint, icon, loading }) {
    return (<Card className="stat-card">
      <div className="stat-label">{icon && <Icon name={icon} size={16}/>}{label}</div>
      {loading ? (<Skeleton width={96} height={32}/>) : (<div className="stat-value">
          {typeof value === 'number' ? <CountUp value={value} decimals={decimals} suffix={suffix}/> : value ?? '—'}
        </div>)}
      {hint && <div className="stat-hint">{hint}</div>}
    </Card>);
}
/* ── Avatar ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function Avatar({ name, size = 32 }) {
    return (<span className="ui-avatar" style={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: Math.round(size * 0.42) }} aria-hidden="true">
      {(name || '?').trim()[0]?.toUpperCase() || '?'}
    </span>);
}
/* ── Switch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function Switch({ checked, onChange, label }) {
    return (<button type="button" role="switch" aria-checked={checked} className="switch-row" onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <span className={cx('switch', checked && 'on')} aria-hidden="true"><i /></span>
    </button>);
}
