FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/api.js
========================================================================
import { API_URL } from './config';
// Access token lives in memory only. The refresh token is an httpOnly cookie.
let accessToken = null;
export const getToken = () => accessToken;
export const setToken = (t) => { accessToken = t; };
export class ApiError extends Error {
    status;
    code;
    constructor(message, status, code) {
        super(message);
        this.status = status;
        this.code = code;
    }
}
// Concurrent callers (React strict-mode double effects, parallel requests) share ONE refresh call.
// Refresh tokens rotate, so two simultaneous refreshes would race and could revoke the session.
let refreshing = null;
function refresh() {
    if (!refreshing) {
        refreshing = (async () => {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/auth/refresh`, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' });
                const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
                if (res.ok && data.data?.accessToken) {
                    accessToken = data.data.accessToken;
                    return true;
                }
            }
            catch { /* network error */ }
            return false;
        })().finally(() => { refreshing = null; });
    }
    return refreshing;
}
const tokenExpiresInMs = (t) => {
    try {
        const payload = JSON.parse(atob(t.split('.')[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')));
        return payload.exp * 1000 - Date.now();
    }
    catch {
        return 0;
    }
};
/** A token that is valid for at least another minute (refreshes if needed). Used for the socket handshake. */
export async function getFreshToken() {
    if (!accessToken || tokenExpiresInMs(accessToken) < 60_000)
        await refresh();
    return accessToken;
}
// Pick the most useful message from the backend's error shapes.
const errorMessage = (data, status) => {
    if (typeof data?.error === 'string' && data.error)
        return data.error;
    if (Array.isArray(data?.details) && data.details[0]?.msg)
        return data.details[0].msg;
    if (status === 429)
        return 'Too many requests. Please wait a moment and try again.';
    return `HTTP ${status}`;
};
async function request(method, path, body, retried = false) {
    let res;
    try {
        res = await fetch(`${API_URL}${path}`, {
            method,
            credentials: 'include',
            headers: {
                ...(body !== undefined && method !== 'GET' ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}),
                ...(accessToken ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` } : {}),
            },
            body: body !== undefined && method !== 'GET' ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
        });
    }
    catch {
        throw new ApiError('Cannot reach the server. Check your connection and try again.', 0, 'NETWORK_ERROR');
    }
    // Try a silent refresh on 401 — but never for /auth/* calls: a wrong password / wrong OTP is a
    // legitimate 401 and must not trigger (and rotate) a refresh, then replay the request.
    if (res.status === 401 && !retried && !path.startsWith('/auth/') && (await refresh())) {
        return request(method, path, body, true);
    }
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (!res.ok)
        throw new ApiError(errorMessage(data, res.status), res.status, data.code);
    return data;
}
// Login / OTP / Google responses carry the access token; keep it in memory right away.
const withToken = async (p) => {
    const res = await p;
    if (res?.data?.accessToken)
        accessToken = res.data.accessToken;
    return res;
};
const qs = (p = {}) => {
    const s = new URLSearchParams(Object.entries(p).filter(([, v]) => v !== '' && v != null)).toString();
    return s ? `?${s}` : '';
};
export const api = {
    bootstrap: async () => (accessToken ? true : refresh()),
    auth: {
        register: (d) => request('POST', '/auth/register', d),
        login: (d) => withToken(request('POST', '/auth/login', d)),
        logout: () => request('POST', '/auth/logout', {}),
        google: (idToken) => withToken(request('POST', '/auth/google', { idToken })),
        verifyOtp: (d) => withToken(request('POST', '/auth/verify-otp', d)),
        resendOtp: (d) => request('POST', '/auth/resend-otp', d),
        forgotPassword: (d) => request('POST', '/auth/forgot-password', d),
        resetPassword: (token, d) => request('POST', `/auth/reset-password/${token}`, d),
    },
    users: {
        me: () => request('GET', '/users/me'),
        updateProfile: (d) => request('PATCH', '/users/profile', d),
        leaderboard: () => request('GET', '/users/leaderboard'),
    },
    sessions: {
        list: (p = {}) => request('GET', `/sessions${qs(p)}`),
        create: (d) => request('POST', '/sessions', d),
        remove: (id) => request('DELETE', `/sessions/${id}`),
    },
    speech: {
        analyze: (d) => request('POST', '/speech/analyze', d),
        accuracyTrend: (days = 30) => request('GET', `/speech/accuracy-trend?days=${days}`),
    },
    chat: {
        messages: (roomId = 'global', page = 1) => request('GET', `/chat/messages${qs({ roomId, page })}`),
    },
    phrases: {
        daily: (lang, difficulty, refreshNow = false) => request('GET', `/phrases/daily${qs({ lang, difficulty, refresh: refreshNow ? '1' : '' })}`),
    },
    conversation: {
        chat: (d) => request('POST', '/conversation/chat', d),
    },
};

FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/auth-context.jsx
========================================================================
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api, setToken } from './api';
const Ctx = createContext({ user: null, loading: true, reload: async () => null, logout: async () => { } });
export const useAuth = () => useContext(Ctx);
export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
    const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const reload = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            if (!(await api.bootstrap()))
                throw new Error('no session');
            const res = await api.users.me();
            setUser(res.data.user);
            return res.data.user;
        }
        catch {
            setUser(null);
            return null;
        }
        finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    }, []);
    useEffect(() => { reload(); }, [reload]);
    const logout = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            await api.auth.logout();
        }
        catch { /* ignore */ }
        setToken(null);
        setUser(null);
        window.location.href = '/';
    }, []);
    return <Ctx.Provider value={{ user, loading, reload, logout }}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/firebase.js

// Firebase client SDK — used ONLY for Google sign-in.
// The app's own session (JWT cookies) is still issued by the backend after it verifies the ID token.
//
// Everything is initialised lazily inside getGoogleIdToken(). Initialising at import time made the
// whole landing page crash (and `next build` fail) whenever VITE_FIREBASE_* was missing,
// even for people who only use email/password.
import { initializeApp, getApps, getApp } from 'firebase/app';
import { getAuth, GoogleAuthProvider, signInWithPopup, signOut } from 'firebase/auth';
const firebaseConfig = {
    apiKey: import.meta.env.VITE_FIREBASE_API_KEY,
    authDomain: import.meta.env.VITE_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN,
    projectId: import.meta.env.VITE_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
    appId: import.meta.env.VITE_FIREBASE_APP_ID,
};
/** Opens the Google popup and returns a Firebase ID token for the backend to verify. */
export async function getGoogleIdToken() {
    if (!firebaseConfig.apiKey || !firebaseConfig.projectId) {
        throw new Error('Google sign-in is not configured. Set the VITE_FIREBASE_* variables.');
    }
    const app = getApps().length ? getApp() : initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
    const auth = getAuth(app);
    const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
    provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });
    const cred = await signInWithPopup(auth, provider);
    const idToken = await cred.user.getIdToken();
    // We use our own backend session, so don't keep a second (Firebase) session in the browser.
    await signOut(auth).catch(() => { });
    return idToken;
}

// Used only if the phrase API is unreachable.
export const FALLBACK_PHRASES = {

========================================================================
FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/toast.jsx
========================================================================
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useState } from 'react';
const Ctx = createContext(() => { });
export const useToast = () => useContext(Ctx);
export function ToastProvider({ children }) {
    const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
    const push = useCallback((msg, type = 'info') => {
        const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
        setItems((s) => [...s, { id, msg, type }]);
        setTimeout(() => setItems((s) => s.filter((t) => t.id !== id)), 4000);
    }, []);
    return (<Ctx.Provider value={push}>
      {children}
      <div className="toasts" aria-live="polite">
        {items.map((t) => (<div key={t.id} className={`toast ${t.type}`}>{t.msg}</div>))}
      </div>
    </Ctx.Provider>);
}

========================================================================
FILE: frontend-react/src/lib/useSpeech.js
========================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
/** Thin wrapper around the browser Web Speech API. */
export function useSpeechRecognition({ lang, continuous = false, onFinal }) {
    const [supported, setSupported] = useState(true);
    const [listening, setListening] = useState(false);
    const [text, setText] = useState('');
    const recRef = useRef(null);
    const wantRef = useRef(false);
    const finalRef = useRef('');
    const onFinalRef = useRef(onFinal);
    onFinalRef.current = onFinal;
    useEffect(() => {
        const w = window;
        const ios = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent);
        setSupported(!!(w.SpeechRecognition || w.webkitSpeechRecognition) && !ios);
    }, []);
    const stop = useCallback(() => {
        wantRef.current = false;
        try {
            recRef.current?.stop();
        }
        catch { /* ignore */ }
        setListening(false);
    }, []);
    const start = useCallback(async () => {
        const w = window;
        const SR = w.SpeechRecognition || w.webkitSpeechRecognition;
        if (!SR)
            return 'unsupported';
        finalRef.current = '';
        setText('');
        const rec = new SR();
        rec.lang = lang;
        rec.continuous = continuous;
        rec.interimResults = true;
        let error = null;
        rec.onresult = (e) => {
            let interim = '';
            for (let i = e.resultIndex; i < e.results.length; i++) {
                const t = e.results[i][0].transcript;
                if (e.results[i].isFinal) {
                    finalRef.current = (finalRef.current + ' ' + t).trim();
                    onFinalRef.current?.(t.trim());
                }
                else
                    interim += t;
            }
            setText((finalRef.current + ' ' + interim).trim());
        };
        rec.onerror = (e) => {
            if (e.error === 'no-speech' || e.error === 'aborted')
                return;
            error = e.error;
            wantRef.current = false;
            setListening(false);
        };
        rec.onend = () => {
            if (wantRef.current && continuous) {
                try {
                    rec.start();
                }
                catch { /* ignore */ }
            }
            else {
                setListening(false);
            }
        };
        recRef.current = rec;
        wantRef.current = true;
        try {
            rec.start();
            setListening(true);
        }
        catch {
            return 'failed';
        }
        return error;
    }, [lang, continuous]);
    useEffect(() => () => { wantRef.current = false; try {
        recRef.current?.abort();
    }
    catch { /* ignore */ } }, []);
    return { supported, listening, text, setText, start, stop };
}

/* ── Page ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function Landing() {
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const toast = useToast();
    const [params] = useSearchParams();
    const [modal, setModal] = useState(null);
    const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
    const rootRef = useRef(null);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (params.get('error') === 'oauth_failed')
            toast('Google sign-in failed. Please try again.', 'error');
        if (params.get('verified') === '1')
            toast('Email verified. Welcome to LinguaWave!', 'success');
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, []);
    // Fade-up on scroll
    useEffect(() => {
        const root = rootRef.current;
        if (!root)
            return;
        const els = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.lp-reveal'));
        const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
        if (reduce || typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') {
            els.forEach((el) => el.classList.add('in'));
            return;
        }
        const io = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
            entries.forEach((e) => {
                if (e.isIntersecting) {
                    e.target.classList.add('in');
                    io.unobserve(e.target);
                }
            });
        }, { threshold: 0.12, rootMargin: '0px 0px -6% 0px' });
        els.forEach((el) => io.observe(el));
        setReady(true);
        return () => io.disconnect();
    }, []);
    return (<>
      <div ref={rootRef} className={`landing${ready ? ' lp-ready' : ''}`}>
        <header className="lp-nav-wrap">
          <div className="lp-nav">
            <Logo variant="neon"/>
            <nav className="lp-links" aria-label="Primary">
              <a href="#features">Features</a>
              <a href="#how">How it works</a>
            </nav>
            <div className="lp-actions">
              {user ? (<Link to="/dashboard" className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary">Dashboard</Link>) : (<>
                  <button className="lp-btn lp-btn-ghost lp-login" onClick={() => setModal('login')}>Log in</button>
                  <button className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary" onClick={() => setModal('register')}>Get started</button>
                </>)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <main>
          <section className="lp-hero" aria-labelledby="hero-title">
            <Aurora side="left"/>
            <Aurora side="right"/>
            <div className="lp-container">
              <h1 id="hero-title" className="lp-h1 lp-reveal">
                <span className="lp-line">Speak any language</span>
                <span className="lp-line">with <em className="lp-grad">confidence<Sparkle /></em></span>
              </h1>
              <p className="lp-sub lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': '100ms' })}>
                Practice out loud and get instant AI feedback on your pronunciation, fluency and intonation.
              </p>
              <div className="lp-cta lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': '200ms' })}>
                {user ? (<Link to="/dashboard" className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary lp-btn-lg">Go to dashboard <Icon name="arrow" size={18}/></Link>) : (<button className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary lp-btn-lg" onClick={() => setModal('register')}>Start practicing free <Icon name="arrow" size={18}/></button>)}
                <a href="#how" className="lp-btn lp-btn-outline lp-btn-lg"><Icon name="play" size={14}/> See how it works</a>
              </div>

              <div className="lp-stage lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': '300ms' })}>
                <span className="lp-orbit" aria-hidden="true"/>
                <span className="lp-orbit two" aria-hidden="true"/>
                <Device />
                {FLOATERS.map((f) => (<span key={f.cls} className={`lp-float lp-${f.kind} ${f.cls}`} style={v({ '--rot': `${f.rot}deg`, '--dur': `${f.dur}s`, '--del': `${f.del}s`, ...(f.glow ? { '--glow': f.glow } : {}) })} aria-hidden="true">
                    {f.text}
                  </span>))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="lp-section" id="features" aria-labelledby="features-title">
            <div className="lp-container">
              <div className="lp-head lp-reveal">
                <h2 id="features-title">Everything you need to <span className="lp-grad">sound natural.</span><Sparkle /></h2>
              </div>
              <div className="lp-bento">
                {FEATURES.map((f, i) => (<article key={f.id} className="lp-glass lp-card lp-feature lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': `${(i % 3) * 90}ms` })}>
                    <div className="lp-feature-head">
                      <span className={`lp-ficon ${f.id}`}><Icon name={f.icon} size={26}/></span>
                      <h3>{f.title}</h3>
                    </div>
                    <p>{f.text}</p>
                    <div className="lp-art"><FeatureArt id={f.id}/></div>
                  </article>))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="lp-section" id="how" aria-labelledby="how-title">
            <div className="lp-container">
              <div className="lp-head lp-reveal">
                <h2 id="how-title">Three <em className="lp-grad">simple</em> steps<Sparkle /></h2>
              </div>
              <div className="lp-steps">
                {STEPS.map((s, i) => (<article key={s.title} className="lp-glass lp-card lp-step lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': `${i * 110}ms` })}>
                    <span className="lp-step-tile"><Icon name={s.icon} size={30}/></span>
                    <div>
                      <div className="lp-step-title">
                        <span className="lp-badge" aria-hidden="true">{i + 1}</span>
                        <h3>{s.title}</h3>
                      </div>
                      <p>{s.text}</p>
                    </div>
                    {i < STEPS.length - 1 && (<svg className="lp-link" viewBox="0 0 56 28" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                        <path d="M3 14C14-2 22 30 32 14S46 6 53 14"/>
                        <circle cx="3" cy="14" r="3"/>
                        <circle cx="53" cy="14" r="3"/>
                      </svg>)}
                  </article>))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>
        </main>

        <footer className="lp-footer">
          <div className="lp-container">
            <div className="lp-footer-inner">
              <Logo variant="neon"/>
              <span className="lp-vr" aria-hidden="true"/>
              <span>© {new Date().getFullYear()} LinguaWave · AI-powered pronunciation coaching</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </footer>
      </div>

      {modal && <AuthModal initialTab={modal} onClose={() => setModal(null)}/>}
    </>);
}

========================================================================
FILE: frontend-react/src/pages/ResetPassword.jsx
========================================================================
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Suspense, useState } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import Logo from '@/components/Logo';
function ResetForm() {
    const token = useSearchParams()[0].get('token');
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
    const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
    const [msg, setMsg] = useState(null);
    const forgot = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!email.trim())
            return setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Email is required.' });
        setBusy(true);
        setMsg(null);
        try {
            await api.auth.forgotPassword({ email: email.trim() });
            setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Reset link sent. Check your inbox.' });
        }
        catch {
            setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Something went wrong. Try again.' });
        }
        finally {
            setBusy(false);
        }
    };
    const reset = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (password.length < 8)
            return setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Password must be at least 8 characters.' });
        setBusy(true);
        setMsg(null);
        try {
            await api.auth.resetPassword(token, { password });
            setDone(true);
        }
        catch (err) {
            setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Reset failed. The link may have expired.' });
        }
        finally {
            setBusy(false);
        }
    };
    if (done) {
        return (<>
        <h2>Password updated</h2>
        <p className="muted" style={{ margin: '8px 0 20px' }}>You can now log in with your new password.</p>
        <Link to="/" className="btn btn-primary btn-block">Go to login</Link>
      </>);
    }
    return token ? (<form onSubmit={reset} noValidate>
      <h2>Set a new password</h2>
      <p className="muted small" style={{ margin: '4px 0 18px' }}>Choose a strong password for your account.</p>
      {msg && <div className={`form-msg ${msg.type}`}>{msg.text}</div>}
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="np">New password</label>
        <input id="np" type="password" className="input" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="Min. 8 characters"/>
      </div>
      <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner"/> : 'Update password'}</button>
    </form>) : (<form onSubmit={forgot} noValidate>
      <h2>Forgot password?</h2>
      <p className="muted small" style={{ margin: '4px 0 18px' }}>Enter your email and we’ll send you a reset link.</p>
      {msg && <div className={`form-msg ${msg.type}`}>{msg.text}</div>}
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="fe">Email</label>
        <input id="fe" type="email" className="input" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com"/>
      </div>
      <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner"/> : 'Send reset link'}</button>
      <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 14 }} className="small"><Link to="/" className="muted">← Back to home</Link></p>
    </form>);
}
export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
    return (<div style={{ minHeight: '100vh', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', padding: 16 }}>
      <div style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 400 }}>
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 20 }}><Logo /></div>
        <div className="card">
          <Suspense fallback={null}><ResetForm /></Suspense>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>);
}

========================================================================
FILE: frontend-react/src/pages/dashboard/Community.jsx
========================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { io } from 'socket.io-client';
import { api, getFreshToken } from '@/lib/api';
import { LANGUAGES, SOCKET_URL } from '@/lib/config';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';
import { Avatar, Card, Chip, EmptyState, PageHeader, Skeleton } from '@/components/ui';
const flagOf = (code) => LANGUAGES.find((l) => l.code === code)?.flag;
const ROOMS = [
    { id: 'global', label: 'Global', flag: null },
    { id: 'english', label: 'English', flag: flagOf('en') ?? null },
    { id: 'french', label: 'French', flag: flagOf('fr') ?? null },
    { id: 'spanish', label: 'Spanish', flag: flagOf('es') ?? null },
];
const toMsg = (m, myId) => ({
    id: m.id || m._id || `${m.createdAt}-${Math.random()}`,
    own: m.user?.id === myId || m.userId === myId,
    name: m.user?.username || 'Anonymous',
    content: m.content,
    time: new Date(m.createdAt).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
});
export default function CommunityPage() {
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const toast = useToast();
    const [room, setRoom] = useState('global');
    const [messages, setMessages] = useState([]);
    const [input, setInput] = useState('');
    const [online, setOnline] = useState(0);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const socketRef = useRef(null);
    const roomRef = useRef(room);
    const endRef = useRef(null);
    roomRef.current = room;
    // One socket for the page's lifetime.
    useEffect(() => {
        // `auth` as a function is re-evaluated on every (re)connect, so an expired 15-min access token
        // is refreshed instead of failing the handshake forever.
        const socket = io(SOCKET_URL, {
            auth: (cb) => { getFreshToken().then((token) => cb({ token })); },
            transports: ['websocket', 'polling'],
            withCredentials: true,
        });
        socketRef.current = socket;
        socket.on('connect', () => socket.emit('chat:join', roomRef.current));
        socket.on('users:online', (n) => setOnline(n));
        socket.on('chat:message', (m) => setMessages((s) => [...s, toMsg(m, user?.id)]));
        socket.on('chat:userJoined', (d) => setMessages((s) => [...s, { id: `j${Date.now()}`, own: false, name: '', content: `${d.username} joined the room`, time: '', system: true }]));
        socket.on('chat:error', (e) => toast(e?.message || 'Message failed to send', 'error'));
        socket.on('connect_error', () => toast('Could not connect to chat server', 'error'));
        return () => { socket.disconnect(); };
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, []);
    // Load history + switch rooms.
    useEffect(() => {
        setLoading(true);
        socketRef.current?.emit('chat:join', room);
        api.chat.messages(room)
            .then((r) => setMessages((r.data?.messages || []).map((m) => toMsg(m, user?.id))))
            .catch(() => setMessages([]))
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
        return () => { socketRef.current?.emit('chat:leave', room); };
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, [room]);
    useEffect(() => { endRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' }); }, [messages]);
    const send = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const content = input.trim();
        if (!content)
            return;
        socketRef.current?.emit('chat:message', { content, roomId: room });
        setInput('');
    };
    const current = ROOMS.find((r) => r.id === room);
    return (<>
      <PageHeader title="Community" subtitle="Chat with other learners in real time."/>
      <div className="community">
        <Card padded={false} className="rooms-panel">
          <div className="rooms-head">
            <span>Rooms</span>
            <Chip dot>{online} online</Chip>
          </div>
          <nav className="room-list" aria-label="Chat rooms">
            {ROOMS.map((r) => (<button key={r.id} type="button" className={`room ${room === r.id ? 'active' : ''}`} aria-current={room === r.id ? 'true' : undefined} onClick={() => setRoom(r.id)}>
                <span className="room-ico" aria-hidden="true">{r.flag ? r.flag : <Icon name="globe" size={18}/>}</span>
                <span className="room-name">{r.label}</span>
                <i className="room-dot" aria-hidden="true"/>
              </button>))}
          </nav>
        </Card>

        <Card padded={false} className="chat-shell chat-main">
          <div className="chat-head">
            <div className="ch-info">
              <span className="ch-flag" aria-hidden="true">{current.flag ? current.flag : <Icon name="globe" size={18}/>}</span>
              <b>{current.label}</b>
            </div>
            <Chip dot>{online} online</Chip>
          </div>

          <div className="messages" role="log" aria-live="polite" aria-label={`${current.label} room messages`}>
            {loading ? (<div className="skel-list"><Skeleton height={44} radius={12} width="60%"/><Skeleton height={44} radius={12} width="45%"/><Skeleton height={44} radius={12} width="55%"/></div>) : messages.length === 0 ? (<EmptyState icon="users" message="No messages yet. Say hello!"/>) : (messages.map((m) => m.system ? (<div key={m.id} className="system-msg">{m.content}</div>) : (<div key={m.id} className={`cmsg ${m.own ? 'own' : ''}`}>
                  {!m.own && <Avatar name={m.name} size={32}/>}
                  <div className="cmsg-body">
                    <div className="cmsg-meta">{!m.own && <b>{m.name}</b>}<time>{m.time}</time></div>
                    <div className="bubble">{m.content}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>)))}
            <div ref={endRef}/>
          </div>

          <form className="composer" onSubmit={send}>
            <input className="input" value={input} onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)} placeholder="Message the community…" maxLength={2000} aria-label="Message"/>
            <button type="submit" className="icon-btn primary" aria-label="Send" disabled={!input.trim()}>
              <Icon name="send" size={20}/>
            </button>
          </form>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </>);
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/pages/dashboard/DashboardLayout.jsx

import '@/styles/dashboard.css';
import { Link, Outlet, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Logo from '@/components/Logo';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';
import { Avatar, Chip, Skeleton } from '@/components/ui';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
const NAV = [
    { href: '/dashboard', label: 'Practice', icon: 'mic' },
    { href: '/dashboard/tutor', label: 'AI Tutor', icon: 'sparkle' },
    { href: '/dashboard/progress', label: 'Progress', icon: 'bar-chart' },
    { href: '/dashboard/community', label: 'Community', icon: 'users' },
    { href: '/dashboard/sessions', label: 'Sessions', icon: 'calendar' },
    { href: '/dashboard/leaderboard', label: 'Leaderboard', icon: 'trophy' },
];
export default function DashboardLayout() {
    const { user, loading, logout } = useAuth();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { pathname } = useLocation();
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    useEffect(() => { if (!loading && !user)
        navigate('/', { replace: true }); }, [loading, user, navigate]);
    useEffect(() => setOpen(false), [pathname]);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!open)
            return;
        const onKey = (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape')
            setOpen(false); };
        window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
        return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    }, [open]);
    if (loading || !user) {
        return (<div className="dash page-loading" role="status" aria-label="Loading">
        <div className="pl-inner">
          <Skeleton width={160} height={28}/>
          <Skeleton width="100%" height={180} radius={12}/>
          <Skeleton width="100%" height={120} radius={12}/>
        </div>
      </div>);
    }
    const found = NAV.findIndex((n) => (n.href === '/dashboard' ? pathname === n.href : pathname.startsWith(n.href)));
    const activeIdx = found === -1 ? 0 : found;
    const streak = user.activity?.streak?.current ?? 0;
    return (<div className="dash shell">
      <div className={`backdrop ${open ? 'open' : ''}`} onClick={() => setOpen(false)} aria-hidden="true"/>
      <aside id="sidebar" className={`sidebar ${open ? 'open' : ''}`} aria-label="Sidebar">
        <Logo href="/"/>
        <nav className="side-nav" aria-label="Dashboard" style={{ '--i': activeIdx }}>
          <span className="side-pill" aria-hidden="true"/>
          {NAV.map((n, i) => (<Link key={n.href} to={n.href} className={`side-link ${i === activeIdx ? 'active' : ''}`} aria-current={i === activeIdx ? 'page' : undefined}>
              <Icon name={n.icon} size={20}/>
              <span>{n.label}</span>
            </Link>))}
        </nav>
        <div className="side-user">
          <Avatar name={user.username} size={36}/>
          <div className="name" title={user.username}>{user.username}</div>
          <button type="button" className="side-logout" onClick={logout} aria-label="Log out">
            <Icon name="logout" size={20}/>
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="main">
        <header className="topbar">
          <button type="button" className="menu-btn" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} aria-label="Open menu" aria-expanded={open} aria-controls="sidebar">
            <Icon name="menu" size={22}/>
          </button>
          <div className="topbar-logo"><Logo href="/"/></div>
          <Chip dot>{streak} day streak</Chip>
        </header>
        <main className="content" key={pathname}><Outlet /></main>
      </div>
    </div>);
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { Avatar, Card, Chip, EmptyState, PageHeader, Skeleton } from '@/components/ui';
export default function LeaderboardPage() {
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const [rows, setRows] = useState(null);
    const [error, setError] = useState(false);
    useEffect(() => {
        api.users.leaderboard().then((r) => setRows(r.data?.leaderboard || [])).catch(() => setError(true));
    }, []);
    return (<>
      <PageHeader title="Leaderboard" subtitle="Top learners by sessions and streak."/>
      <Card padded={false}>
        {error ? <EmptyState icon="trophy" message="Leaderboard unavailable."/>
            : !rows ? <div className="skel-list">{Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} height={56} radius={8}/>)}</div>
                : rows.length === 0 ? <EmptyState icon="trophy" message="No data yet. Be the first!"/>
                    : (<ol className="lb-list">
              {rows.map((u, i) => {
                            const me = !!user && u.userId === user.id;
                            const name = u.username || `User #${String(u.userId || '').slice(-6)}`;
                            return (<li key={u.userId || i} className={`lb-row ${me ? 'me' : ''}`} style={{ '--s': i }}>
                    <span className={`lb-rank r${i + 1}`}>{i + 1}</span>
                    <Avatar name={name} size={36}/>
                    <div className="lb-name">
                      <b>{name}</b>
                      <span className="muted small">{u.totalMinutes || 0} min practiced{me ? ' · You' : ''}</span>
                    </div>
                    <Chip icon="flame">{u.streak?.current || 0}</Chip>
                    <div className="lb-val">{u.totalSessions ?? 0}<small>sessions</small></div>
                  </li>);
                        })}
            </ol>)}
      </Card>
    </>);
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/pages/dashboard/Practice.jsx
========================================================================
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { LANGUAGES } from '@/lib/config';
import { FALLBACK_PHRASES } from '@/lib/phrases';
import { useSpeechRecognition } from '@/lib/useSpeech';
import { useToast } from '@/lib/toast';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';
import { AccuracyLine, CountBars } from '@/components/charts';
import { Avatar, Card, CardTitle, Chip, CountUp, EmptyState, PageHeader, SegmentedControl, Skeleton, Switch } from '@/components/ui';
const LEVELS = ['beginner', 'intermediate', 'advanced'];
/** Score → semantic tone (teal for good, amber, red). */
const tone = (n) => (n >= 80 ? 'good' : n >= 60 ? 'warn' : 'bad');
const clock = (s) => `${String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
const WAVE = Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, i) => 6 + Math.round(Math.abs(Math.sin(i * 1.3) * Math.cos(i * 0.55)) * 34));
const verdict = (n) => n >= 90 ? ['Excellent.', 'Your pronunciation is clear and natural.']
    : n >= 75 ? ['Good job.', 'A few words need more attention.']
        : n >= 60 ? ['Getting there.', 'Focus on the highlighted words.']
            : ['Keep practicing.', 'Try the phrase again, a little slower.'];
function Ring({ value }) {
    const r = 64, c = 2 * Math.PI * r;
    const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value));
    return (<div className={`score-ring t-${tone(pct)}`}>
      <svg width="160" height="160" viewBox="0 0 160 160" aria-hidden="true">
        <circle className="rt" cx="80" cy="80" r={r}/>
        <circle className="rf" cx="80" cy="80" r={r} strokeDasharray={c} strokeDashoffset={c * (1 - pct / 100)} style={{ '--c': c }}/>
      </svg>
      <div className="num"><CountUp value={pct} suffix="%"/></div>
    </div>);
}
function SubBar({ icon, label, v }) {
    const n = v ?? 0;
    return (<div className="sub-row">
      <Icon name={icon} size={18}/>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <div className="mbar"><i className={`t-${tone(n)}`} style={{ '--v': n / 100 }}/></div>
      <b>{v == null ? '—' : `${Math.round(v)}%`}</b>
    </div>);
}
export default function PracticePage() {
    const toast = useToast();
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const [lang, setLang] = useState('en');
    const [level, setLevel] = useState('intermediate');
    const [phrases, setPhrases] = useState([]);
    const [idx, setIdx] = useState(0);
    const [loadingPhrases, setLoadingPhrases] = useState(true);
    const [showTranslation, setShowTranslation] = useState(false);
    const [seconds, setSeconds] = useState(0);
    const [manual, setManual] = useState('');
    const [analyzing, setAnalyzing] = useState(false);
    const [result, setResult] = useState(null);
    const [resultKey, setResultKey] = useState(0);
    const [trend, setTrend] = useState(null);
    const [board, setBoard] = useState(null);
    const speechLang = LANGUAGES.find((l) => l.code === lang)?.speech || 'en-US';
    const speech = useSpeechRecognition({ lang: speechLang, continuous: true });
    const transcript = (speech.supported ? speech.text : manual).trim();
    const phrase = phrases[idx];
    const streak = user?.activity?.streak?.current ?? 0;
    const loadPhrases = useCallback(async (force = false) => {
        setLoadingPhrases(true);
        try {
            const res = await api.phrases.daily(lang, level, force);
            const list = res.data?.phrases || [];
            if (!list.length)
                throw new Error('empty');
            setPhrases(list);
            setIdx(Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length));
        }
        catch {
            const list = FALLBACK_PHRASES[lang] || FALLBACK_PHRASES.en;
            setPhrases(list);
            setIdx(0);
        }
        finally {
            setLoadingPhrases(false);
        }
    }, [lang, level]);
    useEffect(() => { loadPhrases(); }, [loadPhrases]);
    const loadTrend = useCallback(() => {
        api.speech.accuracyTrend(30).then((r) => setTrend(r.data?.trend || [])).catch(() => setTrend([]));
    }, []);
    useEffect(() => {
        loadTrend();
        api.users.leaderboard().then((r) => setBoard(r.data?.leaderboard || [])).catch(() => setBoard([]));
    }, [loadTrend]);
    // Reset attempt whenever the phrase or language changes.
    useEffect(() => {
        speech.stop();
        speech.setText('');
        setManual('');
        setShowTranslation(false);
        setSeconds(0);
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, [idx, lang, level]);
    // Recording timer (auto-stops at 2 min)
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!speech.listening)
            return;
        const t = setInterval(() => setSeconds((s) => s + 1), 1000);
        return () => clearInterval(t);
    }, [speech.listening]);
    useEffect(() => { if (seconds >= 120)
        speech.stop(); }, [seconds, speech]);
    const toggleMic = async () => {
        if (speech.listening)
            return speech.stop();
        setSeconds(0);
        const err = await speech.start();
        if (err === 'not-allowed')
            toast('Microphone permission denied. Allow mic access in your browser.', 'error');
        else if (err)
            toast(`Could not start recording (${err}).`, 'error');
    };
    const move = (d) => phrases.length && setIdx((i) => (i + d + phrases.length) % phrases.length);
    const analyze = async () => {
        if (speech.listening)
            speech.stop();
        if (!transcript || !phrase)
            return;
        setAnalyzing(true);
        try {
            const res = await api.speech.analyze({
                targetText: phrase.text,
                transcribedText: transcript,
                language: lang,
                sessionId: `session_${Date.now()}`,
            });
            const data = res.data;
            setResult(data);
            setResultKey((k) => k + 1);
            const words = transcript.split(/\s+/).length;
            api.sessions.create({
                language: lang,
                duration: seconds,
                accuracy: data.overallAccuracy,
                wordsAttempted: words,
                wordsCorrect: Math.round(words * (data.overallAccuracy / 100)),
                transcript,
                feedback: data.suggestions?.join('; '),
            }).then(loadTrend).catch(() => { });
            toast(`Score: ${Math.round(data.overallAccuracy)}%`, 'success');
        }
        catch (err) {
            toast(err.message || 'Analysis failed. Check your connection.', 'error');
        }
        finally {
            setAnalyzing(false);
        }
    };
    /* ── derived data for the bottom row (existing endpoints only) ── */
    const avg = trend && trend.length ? Math.round(trend.reduce((a, p) => a + p.avgAccuracy, 0) / trend.length) : null;
    const delta = trend && trend.length >= 2 ? Math.round(trend[trend.length - 1].avgAccuracy - trend[0].avgAccuracy) : null;
    const week = useMemo(() => {
        if (!trend)
            return null;
        const counts = new Map(trend.map((p) => [p._id, p.count || 0]));
        const out = [];
        for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {
            const d = new Date();
            d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() - i);
            out.push({ label: d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'short', timeZone: 'UTC' }), count: counts.get(d.toISOString().slice(0, 10)) || 0 });
        }
        return out;
    }, [trend]);
    const weekTotal = week ? week.reduce((a, d) => a + d.count, 0) : 0;
    const fmtDay = (iso) => new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' });
    const topicIcon = phrase?.topic && /travel/i.test(phrase.topic) ? 'plane' : 'globe';
    const [vHead, vBody] = result ? verdict(result.overallAccuracy) : ['', ''];
    return (<>
      <PageHeader title="Practice" actions={<>
            <select className="input lang-select" value={lang} onChange={(e) => setLang(e.target.value)} aria-label="Language">
              {LANGUAGES.map((l) => <option key={l.code} value={l.code}>{l.flag} {l.name}</option>)}
            </select>
            <SegmentedControl ariaLabel="Difficulty level" value={level} onChange={setLevel} options={LEVELS.map((l) => ({ value: l, label: l[0].toUpperCase() + l.slice(1) }))}/>
            <span className="ph-streak"><Chip dot>{streak} day streak</Chip></span>
          </>}/>

      <div className="stagger">
        <div className="practice-top">
          {/* ── Practice card ── */}
          <Card className="practice-card">
            <div className="pc-bar">
              {phrase?.topic ? <Chip icon={topicIcon}>{phrase.topic}</Chip> : <span />}
              {phrase?.translation && <Switch label="Show translation" checked={showTranslation} onChange={setShowTranslation}/>}
            </div>

            <div className="phrase-row">
              <button type="button" className="icon-btn" onClick={() => move(-1)} aria-label="Previous phrase"><Icon name="chevron-left" size={20}/></button>
              <div className="phrase-main" aria-live="polite">
                {loadingPhrases || !phrase ? (<div className="phrase-skel"><Skeleton width="70%" height={32}/><Skeleton width="45%" height={16}/></div>) : (<>
                    <div className="p-text">{phrase.text}</div>
                    {phrase.phonetic && <div className="p-ipa">{phrase.phonetic}</div>}
                    {showTranslation && phrase.translation && <div className="p-trans">{phrase.translation}</div>}
                  </>)}
              </div>
              <button type="button" className="icon-btn" onClick={() => move(1)} aria-label="Next phrase"><Icon name="chevron-right" size={20}/></button>
            </div>

            <div className="phrase-meta">
              <span className="muted small">{phrases.length ? `${idx + 1} / ${phrases.length}` : '—'}</span>
              <button type="button" className="ghost-btn" onClick={() => loadPhrases(true)} disabled={loadingPhrases}>
                <Icon name="refresh" size={16}/> New set
              </button>
            </div>
            {phrase?.tip && <div className="p-tip"><Icon name="sparkle" size={16}/>{phrase.tip}</div>}

            {speech.supported ? (<div className="mic-area">
                <div className={`wave-vis ${speech.listening ? 'live' : ''}`} aria-hidden="true">
                  {WAVE.map((h, i) => <i key={i} style={{ '--h': `${h}px`, '--d': `${(i % 9) * 0.07}s` }}/>)}
                </div>
                <div className="mic-stage">
                  <span className={`halo h1 ${speech.listening ? 'live' : ''}`}/>
                  <span className={`halo h2 ${speech.listening ? 'live' : ''}`}/>
                  <button type="button" className="mic-main" onClick={toggleMic} aria-pressed={speech.listening} aria-label={speech.listening ? 'Stop recording' : 'Start recording'}>
                    <Icon name={speech.listening ? 'stop' : 'mic'} size={30}/>
                  </button>
                </div>
                <div className="wave-vis mirror" aria-hidden="true">
                  {WAVE.map((h, i) => <i key={i} style={{ '--h': `${h}px`, '--d': `${((i + 4) % 9) * 0.07}s` }}/>)}
                </div>
              </div>) : (<div className="manual">
                <p className="small muted">Speech recognition isn’t supported in this browser. Type what you said:</p>
                <textarea className="input" rows={3} value={manual} onChange={(e) => setManual(e.target.value)}/>
              </div>)}

            {speech.supported && (<div className="rec-status" aria-live="polite">
                {speech.listening || seconds > 0 ? <div className="rec-time">{clock(seconds)}</div> : null}
                {speech.listening
                ? <div className="rec-label"><i className="rec-dot"/>Recording</div>
                : <div className="rec-label muted">{seconds > 0 ? 'Stopped' : 'Tap to speak'}</div>}
              </div>)}

            {speech.supported && (<div className="transcript">
                {transcript || <span className="muted">Your speech will appear here…</span>}
              </div>)}

            <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={!transcript || analyzing} onClick={analyze}>
              {analyzing ? <span className="spinner" aria-label="Analyzing"/> : 'Analyze pronunciation'}
            </button>
          </Card>

          {/* ── Score card ── */}
          <Card className="score-card">
            <CardTitle>Pronunciation Score</CardTitle>
            {!result ? (<EmptyState icon="mic" message="Your analysis will appear here after you speak."/>) : (<div key={resultKey} className="score-body">
                <div className="score-top">
                  <Ring value={result.overallAccuracy}/>
                  <p className="verdict"><b>{vHead}</b> {vBody}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="sub-list">
                  <SubBar icon="mic" label="Fluency" v={result.fluencyScore}/>
                  <SubBar icon="activity" label="Intonation" v={result.intonationScore}/>
                  <SubBar icon="layers" label="Stress" v={result.stressScore}/>
                </div>

                {!!result.wordsBreakdown?.length && (<div className="word-chips">
                    {result.wordsBreakdown.map((w, i) => (<span key={i} className={`wc ${w.status}`} title={w.note || ''} style={{ '--s': i }}>
                        <b>{w.word}</b><span>{Math.round(w.score)}%</span>
                      </span>))}
                  </div>)}

                {!!result.suggestions?.length && (<>
                    <div className="sub-h">Suggestions</div>
                    <ul className="plain">{result.suggestions.map((s, i) => <li key={i}>{s}</li>)}</ul>
                  </>)}
              </div>)}
          </Card>
        </div>

        {/* ── Bottom row ── */}
        <div className="practice-bottom">
          <Card>
            <CardTitle icon="target">Accuracy</CardTitle>
            {trend === null ? (<><Skeleton width={120} height={36}/><div style={{ height: 12 }}/><Skeleton height={150} radius={8}/></>) : trend.length === 0 ? (<EmptyState icon="trending-up" message="No data yet. Complete a practice session first."/>) : (<>
                <div className="acc-head">
                  <span className="acc-value"><CountUp value={avg} suffix="%"/></span>
                  {delta !== null && (<span className={`delta ${delta >= 0 ? 'up' : 'down'}`} title="Change from first to latest day in the last 30 days">
                      <Icon name={delta >= 0 ? 'arrow-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={14}/>{delta >= 0 ? '+' : ''}{delta}%
                    </span>)}
                </div>
                <AccuracyLine labels={trend.map((p) => fmtDay(p._id))} data={trend.map((p) => Math.round(p.avgAccuracy))} height={160} zoom/>
              </>)}
          </Card>

          <Card>
            <CardTitle icon="calendar" action={<Chip>{weekTotal} {weekTotal === 1 ? 'session' : 'sessions'}</Chip>}>Sessions this week</CardTitle>
            {week === null ? <Skeleton height={190} radius={8}/> : <CountBars labels={week.map((d) => d.label)} data={week.map((d) => d.count)} height={190}/>}
          </Card>

          <Card>
            <CardTitle icon="trophy" action={<Link to="/dashboard/leaderboard" className="link-quiet">View all</Link>}>Leaderboard</CardTitle>
            {board === null ? (<div className="mini-board"><Skeleton height={32}/><Skeleton height={32}/><Skeleton height={32}/></div>) : board.length === 0 ? (<EmptyState icon="trophy" message="No data yet. Be the first!"/>) : (<ol className="mini-board">
                {board.slice(0, 3).map((u, i) => (<li key={u.userId || i}>
                    <span className="mb-rank">{i + 1}</span>
                    <Avatar name={u.username || 'U'} size={32}/>
                    <span className="mb-name">{u.username || `User #${String(u.userId || '').slice(-6)}`}</span>
                    <span className="mb-val">{u.totalSessions ?? 0} <small>sessions</small></span>
                  </li>))}
              </ol>)}
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>);
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { AccuracyLine, CountBars } from '@/components/charts';
import { Card, CardTitle, EmptyState, PageHeader, SegmentedControl, Skeleton, StatCard } from '@/components/ui';
const RANGES = [7, 30, 90];
const fmtDay = (iso) => new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' });
export default function ProgressPage() {
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const [days, setDays] = useState(30);
    const [trend, setTrend] = useState([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    useEffect(() => {
        setLoading(true);
        api.speech.accuracyTrend(days)
            .then((r) => setTrend(r.data?.trend || []))
            .catch(() => setTrend([]))
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [days]);
    const has = trend.length > 0;
    const total = trend.reduce((a, p) => a + (p.count || 0), 0);
    const avg = has ? Math.round(trend.reduce((a, p) => a + p.avgAccuracy, 0) / trend.length) : null;
    const bestPoint = has ? trend.reduce((b, p) => (p.avgAccuracy > b.avgAccuracy ? p : b), trend[0]) : null;
    const streak = user?.activity?.streak?.current ?? 0;
    const labels = trend.map((p) => fmtDay(p._id));
    return (<>
      <PageHeader title="Progress" subtitle="Track how your pronunciation improves over time." actions={<SegmentedControl ariaLabel="Date range" value={days} onChange={setDays} options={RANGES.map((d) => ({ value: d, label: `${d} days` }))}/>}/>

      <div className="stagger">
        <div className="stats-row">
          <StatCard label="Average accuracy" icon="target" loading={loading} value={avg} suffix="%" hint={`Last ${days} days`}/>
          <StatCard label="Total sessions" icon="calendar" loading={loading} value={has ? total : null} hint={`Last ${days} days`}/>
          <StatCard label="Best day" icon="trophy" loading={loading} value={bestPoint ? Math.round(bestPoint.avgAccuracy) : null} suffix="%" hint={bestPoint ? fmtDay(bestPoint._id) : 'No sessions yet'}/>
          <StatCard label="Current streak" icon="flame" loading={false} value={streak} hint={streak === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}/>
        </div>

        <Card>
          <CardTitle icon="trending-up">Accuracy over time</CardTitle>
          {loading ? <Skeleton height={260} radius={8}/>
            : !has ? <EmptyState icon="trending-up" message="No data yet. Complete a practice session first."/>
                : <AccuracyLine labels={labels} data={trend.map((p) => Math.round(p.avgAccuracy))}/>}
        </Card>

        {(loading || has) && (<Card>
            <CardTitle icon="bar-chart">Sessions per day</CardTitle>
            {loading ? <Skeleton height={260} radius={8}/> : <CountBars labels={labels} data={trend.map((p) => p.count || 0)}/>}
          </Card>)}
      </div>
    </>);
}

FILE: frontend-react/src/pages/dashboard/Sessions.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { LANGUAGES } from '@/lib/config';
import Icon from '@/components/Icon';
import { Card, Chip, EmptyState, PageHeader, scoreTone, Skeleton } from '@/components/ui';
const fmtDur = (s) => `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
const fmtDate = (d) => new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
export default function SessionsPage() {
    const [lang, setLang] = useState('');
    const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    const [sessions, setSessions] = useState([]);
    const [pages, setPages] = useState(1);
    const [state, setState] = useState('loading');
    useEffect(() => {
        setState('loading');
        api.sessions.list({ page, limit: 10, language: lang })
            .then((r) => { setSessions(r.data.sessions); setPages(r.data.pagination?.totalPages || 1); setState('ready'); })
            .catch(() => setState('error'));
    }, [page, lang]);
    return (<>
      <PageHeader title="Practice history" subtitle="Every session you’ve completed." actions={<select className="input lang-select" value={lang} aria-label="Filter by language" onChange={(e) => { setLang(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}>
            <option value="">All languages</option>
            {LANGUAGES.map((l) => <option key={l.code} value={l.code}>{l.flag} {l.name}</option>)}
          </select>}/>

      <Card padded={false} className="table-card">
        {state === 'loading' && (<div className="skel-list">{Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} height={44} radius={8}/>)}</div>)}
        {state === 'error' && <EmptyState icon="calendar" message="Couldn’t load sessions."/>}
        {state === 'ready' && sessions.length === 0 && <EmptyState icon="calendar" message="No sessions yet. Start practicing!"/>}

        {state === 'ready' && sessions.length > 0 && (<>
            <table className="data-table">
              <thead>
                <tr><th>Date</th><th>Language</th><th>Phrase</th><th>Accuracy</th><th className="num">Duration</th></tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {sessions.map((s, i) => {
                const l = LANGUAGES.find((x) => x.code === s.language);
                return (<tr key={s.id || s._id} style={{ '--s': i }}>
                      <td data-label="Date">{fmtDate(s.createdAt)}</td>
                      <td data-label="Language">{l ? <span>{l.flag} {l.name}</span> : s.language}</td>
                      <td data-label="Phrase" className="phrase-cell">
                        <span title={s.transcript || ''}>{s.transcript || <span className="muted">{s.wordsCorrect}/{s.wordsAttempted} words correct</span>}</span>
                      </td>
                      <td data-label="Accuracy"><Chip tone={scoreTone(s.accuracy)}>{Math.round(s.accuracy)}%</Chip></td>
                      <td data-label="Duration" className="num">{fmtDur(s.duration || 0)}</td>
                    </tr>);
            })}
              </tbody>
            </table>

            <div className="table-foot">
              <button type="button" className="icon-btn" aria-label="Previous page" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}>
                <Icon name="chevron-left" size={18}/>
              </button>
              <span className="muted small">Page {page} of {pages}</span>
              <button type="button" className="icon-btn" aria-label="Next page" disabled={page >= pages} onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.min(pages, p + 1))}>
                <Icon name="chevron-right" size={18}/>
              </button>
            </div>
          </>)}
      </Card>
    </>);
}

import { Card, CardTitle, Chip, PageHeader, scoreTone, SegmentedControl } from '@/components/ui';
// Same languages / order as before; flags + speech codes come from the shared config.

const TUTOR_LANGS = TUTOR_ORDER.map((n) => LANGUAGES.find((l) => l.name === n)).filter(Boolean);
const SCENARIOS = [
    { key: 'casual', icon: 'coffee', name: 'Casual chat', desc: 'Everyday conversation', prompt: 'Have a casual, friendly conversation. Use natural, everyday language. Be warm and approachable.' },
    { key: 'interview', icon: 'briefcase', name: 'Job interview', desc: 'Professional language', prompt: 'Simulate a professional job interview. Ask common interview questions. Use formal language.' },

    { key: 'shopping', icon: 'bag', name: 'Shopping', desc: 'Consumer vocabulary', prompt: 'Simulate shopping: prices, sizes, purchases, returns.' },

];
const DIFFICULTY = [
    { key: 'easy', label: 'Easy', prompt: 'Use simple, short sentences and common vocabulary. Be very patient and encouraging.' },
    { key: 'medium', label: 'Medium', prompt: 'Use normal conversational pace and vocabulary. Correct mistakes gently.' },
    { key: 'hard', label: 'Hard', prompt: 'Use complex vocabulary and idioms. Challenge the learner with native-level language.' },
];
const fmtTime = (ts) => new Date(ts).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
const fmtClock = (s) => `${String(Math.floor(s / 60)).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
/** Roving-tabindex arrow-key navigation for radio-style groups. */
function arrowKeys(e, i, len, pick) {
    const k = e.key;
    const next = k === 'ArrowRight' || k === 'ArrowDown' ? (i + 1) % len : k === 'ArrowLeft' || k === 'ArrowUp' ? (i - 1 + len) % len : -1;
    if (next < 0)
        return;
    e.preventDefault();
    pick(next);
    e.currentTarget.parentElement?.children[next]?.focus();
}
/** Tweens to `target` whenever it changes (respects prefers-reduced-motion). */
function useTween(target, ms = 600) {
    const [v, setV] = useState(target ?? 0);
    const from = useRef(target ?? 0);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (target == null)
            return;
        if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
            setV(target);
            from.current = target;
            return;
        }
        const a = from.current;
        const start = performance.now();
        let raf = 0;
        const tick = (now) => {
            const t = Math.min(1, (now - start) / ms);
            const val = a + (target - a) * (1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3));
            setV(val);
            from.current = val;
            if (t < 1)
                raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
            else {
                setV(target);
                from.current = target;
            }
        };
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    }, [target, ms]);
    return v;
}
function Stat({ label, value, tone }) {
    const v = useTween(value);
    return (<div className="tt-stat">
      <span>{label}</span>
      <b className={tone ? `t-${tone}` : ''}>{value == null ? '—' : `${Math.round(v)}%`}</b>
    </div>);
}
const toneOf = (n) => (n >= 80 ? 'good' : n >= 60 ? 'warn' : 'bad');
/** First line stronger, rest quieter — only when the reply already has line breaks. */
function Reply({ text }) {
    const lines = text.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    if (lines.length < 2)
        return <>{text}</>;
    return (<>
      <span className="tt-strong">{lines[0]}</span>
      {lines.slice(1).map((l, i) => <span key={i} className="tt-quiet">{l}</span>)}
    </>);
}
export default function TutorPage() {
    const toast = useToast();
    const [started, setStarted] = useState(false);
    const [leaving, setLeaving] = useState(false);
    const [langName, setLangName] = useState('French');
    const [scenario, setScenario] = useState(SCENARIOS[0]);
    const [diff, setDiff] = useState(DIFFICULTY[1]);
    const [messages, setMessages] = useState([]);
    const [scores, setScores] = useState([]);
    const [input, setInput] = useState('');
    const [thinking, setThinking] = useState(false);
    const [secs, setSecs] = useState(0);
    const endRef = useRef(null);
    const taRef = useRef(null);
    const messagesRef = useRef([]);
    messagesRef.current = messages;
    const lang = TUTOR_LANGS.find((l) => l.name === langName);
    const langIdx = TUTOR_LANGS.findIndex((l) => l.name === langName);
    const scIdx = SCENARIOS.findIndex((s) => s.key === scenario.key);
    const grow = (el) => {
        if (!el)
            return;
        el.style.height = 'auto';
        el.style.height = `${Math.min(el.scrollHeight, 140)}px`;
    };
    const send = async (raw) => {
        const text = raw.trim();
        if (!text || thinking)
            return;
        const history = [...messagesRef.current, { role: 'user', text, ts: Date.now() }];
        setMessages(history);
        setInput('');
        requestAnimationFrame(() => grow(taRef.current));
        setThinking(true);
        window.speechSynthesis?.cancel();
        try {
            const res = await api.conversation.chat({
                userMessage: text,
                language: langName,
                conversationHistory: messagesRef.current.slice(-6).map((m) => ({ role: m.role, text: m.text })),
                systemPrompt: [
                    `You are an expert ${langName} language tutor having a live practice conversation.`,
                    `SCENARIO: ${scenario.prompt}`,
                    `DIFFICULTY: ${diff.prompt}`,
                    `Always respond in ${langName}. Keep responses concise.`,
                ].join('\n'),
            });
            const { reply, translation, correction, score } = res.data;
            setMessages((m) => {
                const next = [...m];
                if (typeof score === 'number') {
                    for (let i = next.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                        if (next[i].role === 'user') {
                            next[i] = { ...next[i], score };
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
                return [...next, { role: 'ai', text: reply, translation, correction, ts: Date.now() }];
            });
            if (typeof score === 'number')
                setScores((s) => [...s, score]);
            if (window.speechSynthesis) {
                const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(reply);
                u.lang = lang.speech;
                u.rate = 0.85;
                window.speechSynthesis.speak(u);
            }
        }
        catch (err) {
            toast(err.message || 'The tutor is unavailable right now.', 'error');
        }
        finally {
            setThinking(false);
        }
    };
    const speech = useSpeechRecognition({ lang: lang.speech, onFinal: (t) => send(t) });
    useEffect(() => { endRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'end' }); }, [messages, thinking]);
    useEffect(() => () => window.speechSynthesis?.cancel(), []);
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!started)
            return;
        const t = setInterval(() => setSecs((s) => s + 1), 1000);
        return () => clearInterval(t);
    }, [started]);
    const reset = () => {
        window.speechSynthesis?.cancel();
        speech.stop();
        setMessages([]);
        setScores([]);
        setInput('');
        setSecs(0);
        setStarted(false);
    };
    const newConversation = () => {
        if (started)
            return reset();
        setLangName('French');
        setScenario(SCENARIOS[0]);
        setDiff(DIFFICULTY[1]);
    };
    const begin = () => {
        if (leaving)
            return;
        setLeaving(true);
        setTimeout(() => {
            setMessages([{ role: 'ai', text: `Hello! I’m your ${langName} tutor. Tap the mic or type to start — I’ll reply and help you improve.`, ts: Date.now() }]);
            setScores([]);
            setSecs(0);
            setStarted(true);
            setLeaving(false);
        }, 220);
    };
    const toggleMic = async () => {
        if (speech.listening)
            return speech.stop();
        const err = await speech.start();
        if (err === 'not-allowed')
            toast('Microphone permission denied. Allow mic access in your browser.', 'error');
        else if (err)
            toast(`Could not start recording (${err}).`, 'error');
    };
    const header = (<PageHeader title="AI Tutor" actions={<button type="button" className="btn btn-outline btn-sm tt-new" onClick={newConversation}>
          <Icon name="plus" size={16}/> New conversation
        </button>}/>);
    /* ───────────── Setup ───────────── */
    if (!started) {
        return (<div className={`tutor ${leaving ? 'is-leaving' : ''}`}>
        {header}
        <Card className="tt-setup">
          <h2>Start a conversation</h2>
          <p className="tt-lead">Choose a language, a scenario and a difficulty to begin.</p>

          <div className="tt-label" id="tt-lang-label">Language</div>
          <div className="tt-select">
            <span className="tt-flag" aria-hidden="true">{lang.flag}</span>
            <select value={langName} onChange={(e) => setLangName(e.target.value)} aria-labelledby="tt-lang-label">
              {TUTOR_LANGS.map((l) => <option key={l.name} value={l.name}>{l.name}</option>)}
            </select>
            <span className="tt-chev" aria-hidden="true"><Icon name="chevron-down" size={18}/></span>
          </div>
          <div className="tt-chips" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Language shortcuts">
            {TUTOR_LANGS.map((l, i) => (<button key={l.name} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={l.name === langName} tabIndex={l.name === langName ? 0 : -1} className={`tt-chip ${l.name === langName ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setLangName(l.name)} onKeyDown={(e) => arrowKeys(e, i, TUTOR_LANGS.length, (n) => setLangName(TUTOR_LANGS[n].name))}>
                <span aria-hidden="true">{l.flag}</span>{l.name}
              </button>))}
          </div>

          <div className="tt-label" id="tt-sc-label">Scenario</div>
          <div className="tt-grid" role="radiogroup" aria-labelledby="tt-sc-label">
            {SCENARIOS.map((s, i) => (<button key={s.key} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={scenario.key === s.key} tabIndex={scenario.key === s.key ? 0 : -1} className={`tt-sc ${scenario.key === s.key ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={() => setScenario(s)} onKeyDown={(e) => arrowKeys(e, i, SCENARIOS.length, (n) => setScenario(SCENARIOS[n]))}>
                <Icon name={s.icon} size={24}/>
                <span className="tt-sc-text"><b>{s.name}</b><span>{s.desc}</span></span>
              </button>))}
          </div>

          <div className="tt-label" id="tt-diff-label">Difficulty</div>
          <div className="tt-diff">
            <SegmentedControl ariaLabel="Difficulty" value={diff.key} onChange={(k) => setDiff(DIFFICULTY.find((d) => d.key === k))} options={DIFFICULTY.map((d) => ({ value: d.key, label: d.label }))}/>
          </div>

          <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary btn-lg btn-block tt-cta" onClick={begin}>
            Start conversation <Icon name="arrow-right" size={20}/>
          </button>
        </Card>
      </div>);
    }
    /* ───────────── Chat ───────────── */
    const last = scores.length ? scores[scores.length - 1] : null;
    const avg = scores.length ? Math.round(scores.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / scores.length) : null;
    const best = scores.length ? Math.max(...scores) : null;
    return (<div className="tutor">
      {header}
      <div className="tt-layout tt-stagger">
        <Card padded={false} className="tt-chat" style={{ '--s': 0 }}>
          <div className="tt-bar">
            <label className="tt-pill tt-langpill">
              <span aria-hidden="true">{lang.flag}</span>
              <select value={langName} onChange={(e) => setLangName(e.target.value)} aria-label="Conversation language">
                {TUTOR_LANGS.map((l) => <option key={l.name} value={l.name}>{l.name}</option>)}
              </select>
              <Icon name="chevron-down" size={16}/>
            </label>
            <span className="tt-divider" aria-hidden="true"/>
            <span className="tt-pill"><Icon name={scenario.icon} size={16}/>{scenario.name}</span>
            <span className="tt-pill"><Icon name="bar-chart" size={16}/>{diff.label}</span>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-outline btn-sm tt-end" onClick={reset}>
              <Icon name="stop" size={14}/> End
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="tt-msgs" role="log" aria-live="polite" aria-label="Conversation">
            {messages.map((m, i) => m.role === 'ai' ? (<div key={i} className="tt-row ai">
                <span className="tt-bot" aria-hidden="true"><Icon name="bot" size={20}/></span>
                <div className="tt-col">
                  <div className="tt-bubble">
                    <Reply text={m.text}/>
                    {m.correction && <span className="tt-quiet">{m.correction}</span>}
                  </div>
                  {m.translation && <div className="tt-meta tt-trans">{m.translation}</div>}
                  <div className="tt-meta"><time>{fmtTime(m.ts)}</time></div>
                </div>
              </div>) : (<div key={i} className="tt-row user">
                <div className="tt-col">
                  <div className="tt-bubble">{m.text}</div>
                  <div className="tt-meta">
                    {typeof m.score === 'number' && <span className="tt-pop"><Chip tone={scoreTone(m.score)}>{Math.round(m.score)}%</Chip></span>}
                    <time>{fmtTime(m.ts)}</time>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>))}
            {thinking && (<div className="tt-row ai" aria-label="Tutor is typing">
                <span className="tt-bot" aria-hidden="true"><Icon name="bot" size={20}/></span>
                <div className="tt-col"><div className="tt-bubble tt-dots"><i /><i /><i /></div></div>
              </div>)}
            <div ref={endRef}/>
          </div>

          <form className="tt-composer" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); send(input); }}>
            <div className="tt-field">
              <textarea ref={taRef} rows={1} value={input} onChange={(e) => { setInput(e.target.value); grow(e.currentTarget); }} onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault();
        send(input);
    } }} placeholder={speech.listening ? 'Listening…' : 'Type your message…'} aria-label="Message"/>
              {speech.supported && (<button type="button" className={`tt-mic ${speech.listening ? 'on' : ''}`} onClick={toggleMic} aria-pressed={speech.listening} aria-label={speech.listening ? 'Stop recording' : 'Speak your message'}>
                  <i className="tt-ripple"/><i className="tt-ripple r2"/>
                  <Icon name="mic" size={18}/>
                </button>)}
            </div>
            <button type="submit" className="tt-send" aria-label="Send message" disabled={!input.trim() || thinking}>
              <Icon name="send" size={20}/>
            </button>
          </form>
        </Card>

        <div className="tt-side">
          <Card className="tt-scores" style={{ '--s': 1 }}>
            <CardTitle>Pronunciation scores</CardTitle>
            {scores.length === 0 ? (<p className="muted small">Your pronunciation scores will appear here as you chat.</p>) : (<>
                <div className="tt-stats">
                  <Stat label="Last" value={last} tone={last != null && last >= 80 ? 'good' : last != null ? toneOf(last) : null}/>
                  <Stat label="Avg" value={avg}/>
                  <Stat label="Best" value={best} tone={best != null && best >= 80 ? 'good' : best != null ? toneOf(best) : null}/>
                </div>
                {scores.length > 1 ? (<div className="tt-chart">
                    <AccuracyLine labels={scores.map((_, i) => `#${i + 1}`)} data={scores} label="Score" height={200} zoom highlightLast/>
                  </div>) : (<p className="muted small tt-hint">Send one more message to see your trend.</p>)}
              </>)}
          </Card>

          <Card className="tt-session" style={{ '--s': 2 }}>
            <CardTitle>Session</CardTitle>
            <ul className="tt-rows">
              <li><Icon name="globe" size={18}/><span>Language</span><b>{lang.name}</b></li>
              <li><Icon name="layers" size={18}/><span>Scenario</span><b>{scenario.name}</b></li>
              <li><Icon name="bar-chart" size={18}/><span>Difficulty</span><b>{diff.label}</b></li>
              <li><Icon name="message" size={18}/><span>Message count</span><b>{messages.length}</b></li>
              <li><Icon name="clock" size={18}/><span>Duration</span><b>{fmtClock(secs)}</b></li>
            </ul>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>);
}

   legacy global tokens (--accent, --border, --good …) are remapped, so
   existing pages inherit the new look automatically.

import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  resolve: {
    alias: { '@': fileURLToPath(new URL('./src', import.meta.url)) },
  },
  server: { port: 3000, host: true },
  preview: { port: 3000, host: true, allowedHosts: true },
});
