/* ── Page ────────────────────────────────────────────── */

function Landing() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const toast = useToast();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const [modal, setModal] = useState<null | 'login' | 'register'>(null);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (params.get('error') === 'oauth_failed') toast('Google sign-in failed. Please try again.', 'error');
    if (params.get('verified') === '1') toast('Email verified. Welcome to LinguaWave!', 'success');
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  // Fade-up on scroll
  useEffect(() => {
    const root = rootRef.current;
    if (!root) return;
    const els = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.lp-reveal'));
    const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    if (reduce || typeof IntersectionObserver === 'undefined') {
      els.forEach((el) => el.classList.add('in'));
      return;
    }
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        entries.forEach((e) => {
          if (e.isIntersecting) {
            e.target.classList.add('in');
            io.unobserve(e.target);
          }
        });
      },
      { threshold: 0.12, rootMargin: '0px 0px -6% 0px' },
    );
    els.forEach((el) => io.observe(el));
    setReady(true);
    return () => io.disconnect();
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <div ref={rootRef} className={`landing${ready ? ' lp-ready' : ''}`}>
        <header className="lp-nav-wrap">
          <div className="lp-nav">
            <Logo variant="neon" />
            <nav className="lp-links" aria-label="Primary">
              <a href="#features">Features</a>
              <a href="#how">How it works</a>
            </nav>
            <div className="lp-actions">
              {user ? (
                <Link href="/dashboard" className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary">Dashboard</Link>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <button className="lp-btn lp-btn-ghost lp-login" onClick={() => setModal('login')}>Log in</button>
                  <button className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary" onClick={() => setModal('register')}>Get started</button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <main>
          <section className="lp-hero" aria-labelledby="hero-title">
            <Aurora side="left" />
            <Aurora side="right" />
            <div className="lp-container">
              <h1 id="hero-title" className="lp-h1 lp-reveal">
                <span className="lp-line">Speak any language</span>
                <span className="lp-line">with <em className="lp-grad">confidence<Sparkle /></em></span>
              </h1>
              <p className="lp-sub lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': '100ms' })}>
                Practice out loud and get instant AI feedback on your pronunciation, fluency and intonation.
              </p>
              <div className="lp-cta lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': '200ms' })}>
                {user ? (
                  <Link href="/dashboard" className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary lp-btn-lg">Go to dashboard <Icon name="arrow" size={18} /></Link>
                ) : (
                  <button className="lp-btn lp-btn-primary lp-btn-lg" onClick={() => setModal('register')}>Start practicing free <Icon name="arrow" size={18} /></button>
                )}
                <a href="#how" className="lp-btn lp-btn-outline lp-btn-lg"><Icon name="play" size={14} /> See how it works</a>
              </div>

              <div className="lp-stage lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': '300ms' })}>
                <span className="lp-orbit" aria-hidden="true" />
                <span className="lp-orbit two" aria-hidden="true" />
                <Device />
                {FLOATERS.map((f) => (
                  <span
                    key={f.cls}
                    className={`lp-float lp-${f.kind} ${f.cls}`}
                    style={v({ '--rot': `${f.rot}deg`, '--dur': `${f.dur}s`, '--del': `${f.del}s`, ...(f.glow ? { '--glow': f.glow } : {}) })}
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  >
                    {f.text}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="lp-section" id="features" aria-labelledby="features-title">
            <div className="lp-container">
              <div className="lp-head lp-reveal">
                <h2 id="features-title">Everything you need to <span className="lp-grad">sound natural.</span><Sparkle /></h2>
              </div>
              <div className="lp-bento">
                {FEATURES.map((f, i) => (
                  <article key={f.id} className="lp-glass lp-card lp-feature lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': `${(i % 3) * 90}ms` })}>
                    <div className="lp-feature-head">
                      <span className={`lp-ficon ${f.id}`}><Icon name={f.icon} size={26} /></span>
                      <h3>{f.title}</h3>
                    </div>
                    <p>{f.text}</p>
                    <div className="lp-art"><FeatureArt id={f.id} /></div>
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="lp-section" id="how" aria-labelledby="how-title">
            <div className="lp-container">
              <div className="lp-head lp-reveal">
                <h2 id="how-title">Three <em className="lp-grad">simple</em> steps<Sparkle /></h2>
              </div>
              <div className="lp-steps">
                {STEPS.map((s, i) => (
                  <article key={s.title} className="lp-glass lp-card lp-step lp-reveal" style={v({ '--rd': `${i * 110}ms` })}>
                    <span className="lp-step-tile"><Icon name={s.icon} size={30} /></span>
                    <div>
                      <div className="lp-step-title">
                        <span className="lp-badge" aria-hidden="true">{i + 1}</span>
                        <h3>{s.title}</h3>
                      </div>
                      <p>{s.text}</p>
                    </div>
                    {i < STEPS.length - 1 && (
                      <svg className="lp-link" viewBox="0 0 56 28" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
                        <path d="M3 14C14-2 22 30 32 14S46 6 53 14" />
                        <circle cx="3" cy="14" r="3" />
                        <circle cx="53" cy="14" r="3" />
                      </svg>
                    )}
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>
        </main>

        <footer className="lp-footer">
          <div className="lp-container">
            <div className="lp-footer-inner">
              <Logo variant="neon" />
              <span className="lp-vr" aria-hidden="true" />
              <span>© {new Date().getFullYear()} LinguaWave · AI-powered pronunciation coaching</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </footer>
      </div>

      {modal && <AuthModal initialTab={modal} onClose={() => setModal(null)} />}
    </>
  );
}

export default function Home() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={null}>
      <Landing />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

========================================================================
FILE: frontend/app/reset-password/page.tsx
========================================================================
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { Suspense, useState } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import Logo from '@/components/Logo';

function ResetForm() {
  const token = useSearchParams().get('token');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<{ type: 'error' | 'success'; text: string } | null>(null);

  const forgot = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email.trim()) return setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Email is required.' });
    setBusy(true); setMsg(null);
    try {
      await api.auth.forgotPassword({ email: email.trim() });
      setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'Reset link sent. Check your inbox.' });
    } catch { setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Something went wrong. Try again.' }); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  const reset = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (password.length < 8) return setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Password must be at least 8 characters.' });
    setBusy(true); setMsg(null);
    try {
      await api.auth.resetPassword(token!, { password });
      setDone(true);
    } catch (err: any) { setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Reset failed. The link may have expired.' }); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  if (done) {
    return (
      <>
        <h2>Password updated</h2>
        <p className="muted" style={{ margin: '8px 0 20px' }}>You can now log in with your new password.</p>
        <Link href="/" className="btn btn-primary btn-block">Go to login</Link>
      </>
    );
  }

  return token ? (
    <form onSubmit={reset} noValidate>
      <h2>Set a new password</h2>
      <p className="muted small" style={{ margin: '4px 0 18px' }}>Choose a strong password for your account.</p>
      {msg && <div className={`form-msg ${msg.type}`}>{msg.text}</div>}
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="np">New password</label>
        <input id="np" type="password" className="input" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="Min. 8 characters" />
      </div>
      <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner" /> : 'Update password'}</button>
    </form>
  ) : (
    <form onSubmit={forgot} noValidate>
      <h2>Forgot password?</h2>
      <p className="muted small" style={{ margin: '4px 0 18px' }}>Enter your email and we’ll send you a reset link.</p>
      {msg && <div className={`form-msg ${msg.type}`}>{msg.text}</div>}
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor="fe">Email</label>
        <input id="fe" type="email" className="input" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com" />
      </div>
      <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner" /> : 'Send reset link'}</button>
      <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 14 }} className="small"><Link href="/" className="muted">← Back to home</Link></p>
    </form>
  );
}

export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  return (
    <div style={{ minHeight: '100vh', display: 'grid', placeItems: 'center', padding: 16 }}>
      <div style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 400 }}>
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 20 }}><Logo /></div>
        <div className="card">
          <Suspense fallback={null}><ResetForm /></Suspense>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

========================================================================
FILE: frontend/components/AuthModal.tsx
========================================================================
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { getGoogleIdToken } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';

type Tab = 'login' | 'register' | 'otp';

const emailOk = (v: string) => /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(v);

function strength(pw: string) {
  let s = 0;
  if (pw.length >= 8) s++;
  if (pw.length >= 12) s++;
  if (/[A-Z]/.test(pw)) s++;
  if (/[0-9]/.test(pw)) s++;
  if (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(pw)) s++;
  if (s <= 1) return { pct: 25, color: '#FB7185', label: 'Weak' };
  if (s <= 2) return { pct: 50, color: '#FBBF24', label: 'Fair' };
  if (s <= 3) return { pct: 75, color: '#4ADE80', label: 'Good' };
  return { pct: 100, color: '#2DD4BF', label: 'Strong' };
}

export default function AuthModal({ initialTab, onClose }: { initialTab: 'login' | 'register'; onClose: () => void }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { reload } = useAuth();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>(initialTab);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<{ type: 'error' | 'success'; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPw, setShowPw] = useState(false);

  const [preAuth, setPreAuth] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [masked, setMasked] = useState('');
  const [digits, setDigits] = useState<string[]>(Array(6).fill(''));
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState(0);
  const otpRefs = useRef<(HTMLInputElement | null)[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === 'Escape' && onClose();
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (countdown <= 0) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setCountdown((c) => c - 1), 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [countdown]);

  const switchTab = (t: Tab) => { setTab(t); setMsg(null); setErrors({}); };

  const enterOtp = (token: string, maskedEmail: string) => {
    setPreAuth(token);
    setMasked(maskedEmail);
    setDigits(Array(6).fill(''));
    setCountdown(60);
    setTab('otp');
    setMsg(null);
    setTimeout(() => otpRefs.current[0]?.focus(), 100);
  };

  const finishLogin = async () => {
    await reload();
    router.push('/dashboard');
  };

  const continueWithGoogle = async () => {
    setBusy(true); setMsg(null);
    try {
      const idToken = await getGoogleIdToken();
      await api.auth.google(idToken);
      await finishLogin();
    } catch (err: any) {
      // user closed the popup / opened another one — not an error worth showing
      if (err?.code === 'auth/popup-closed-by-user' || err?.code === 'auth/cancelled-popup-request') return;
      const text = err?.code === 'auth/popup-blocked'
        ? 'Your browser blocked the Google popup. Allow popups and try again.'
        : err?.message || 'Google sign-in failed. Please try again.';
      setMsg({ type: 'error', text });
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  const submitLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const errs: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (!emailOk(email)) errs.email = 'Enter a valid email';
    if (!password) errs.password = 'Password is required';
    setErrors(errs);
    if (Object.keys(errs).length) return;
    setBusy(true); setMsg(null);
    try {
      const res: any = await api.auth.login({ email, password });
      if (res.requiresOtp) return enterOtp(res.preAuthToken, res.maskedEmail || email);
      await finishLogin();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Invalid credentials' });
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  const submitRegister = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const errs: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (!emailOk(email)) errs.email = 'Enter a valid email';
    if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{3,20}$/.test(username)) errs.username = '3–20 letters, numbers or underscores';
    if (password.length < 8) errs.password = 'At least 8 characters';
    setErrors(errs);
    if (Object.keys(errs).length) return;
    setBusy(true); setMsg(null);
    try {
      const res: any = await api.auth.register({ email, username, password });
      if (res.requiresOtp) return enterOtp(res.preAuthToken, res.maskedEmail || email);
      await finishLogin();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Registration failed' });
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  const submitOtp = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const otp = digits.join('');
    if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(otp)) return setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Enter all 6 digits.' });
    if (!preAuth) { setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Session expired. Please log in again.' }); return switchTab('login'); }
    setBusy(true); setMsg(null);
    try {
      await api.auth.verifyOtp({ preAuthToken: preAuth, otp });
      await finishLogin();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setDigits(Array(6).fill(''));
      otpRefs.current[0]?.focus();
      if (err.code === 'OTP_EXPIRED' || err.code === 'PRE_AUTH_EXPIRED') {
        setMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'Your code expired. Please log in again.' });
        setTimeout(() => switchTab('login'), 1800);
      } else setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Incorrect code.' });
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  const resend = async () => {
    if (!preAuth || countdown > 0) return;
    try {
      await api.auth.resendOtp({ preAuthToken: preAuth });
      setMsg({ type: 'success', text: 'New code sent. Check your email.' });
      setCountdown(60);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setMsg({ type: 'error', text: err.message || 'Could not resend code.' });
    }
  };

  const onDigit = (i: number, v: string) => {
    const d = v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-1);
    setDigits((s) => s.map((x, idx) => (idx === i ? d : x)));
    if (d && i < 5) otpRefs.current[i + 1]?.focus();
  };

  const onPaste = (e: React.ClipboardEvent) => {
    const pasted = e.clipboardData.getData('text').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6);
    if (!pasted) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    setDigits(Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => pasted[i] || ''));
    otpRefs.current[Math.min(pasted.length, 5)]?.focus();
  };

  const st = strength(password);

  return (
    <div className="overlay" onMouseDown={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()} role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
      <div className="modal">
        <button className="modal-close" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">×</button>

        {tab !== 'otp' && (
          <div className="tabs">
            <button className={`tab ${tab === 'login' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => switchTab('login')}>Log in</button>
            <button className={`tab ${tab === 'register' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => switchTab('register')}>Sign up</button>
          </div>
        )}

        {msg && <div className={`form-msg ${msg.type}`}>{msg.text}</div>}

        {tab === 'login' && (
          <form onSubmit={submitLogin} noValidate>
            <h2>Welcome back</h2>
            <p className="muted small" style={{ marginBottom: 18 }}>Continue your language journey.</p>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="le">Email</label>
              <input id="le" className="input" type="email" autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com" />
              <span className="field-error">{errors.email}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="lp">Password</label>
              <div className="pw-wrap">
                <input id="lp" className="input" type={showPw ? 'text' : 'password'} autoComplete="current-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
                <button type="button" className="pw-toggle" onClick={() => setShowPw((s) => !s)}>{showPw ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}</button>
              </div>
              <span className="field-error">{errors.password}</span>
            </div>
            <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner" /> : 'Log in'}</button>
            <div className="divider">or</div>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-outline btn-block" disabled={busy} onClick={continueWithGoogle}>Continue with Google</button>
            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 14 }} className="small">
              <Link href="/reset-password" className="muted">Forgot password?</Link>
            </p>
          </form>
        )}

        {tab === 'register' && (
          <form onSubmit={submitRegister} noValidate>
            <h2>Create your account</h2>
            <p className="muted small" style={{ marginBottom: 18 }}>Start speaking better today.</p>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="re">Email</label>
              <input id="re" className="input" type="email" autoComplete="email" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com" />
              <span className="field-error">{errors.email}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="ru">Username</label>
              <input id="ru" className="input" value={username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} placeholder="linguastar" maxLength={20} />
              <span className="field-error">{errors.username}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="rp">Password</label>
              <div className="pw-wrap">
                <input id="rp" className="input" type={showPw ? 'text' : 'password'} autoComplete="new-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} placeholder="Min. 8 characters" />
                <button type="button" className="pw-toggle" onClick={() => setShowPw((s) => !s)}>{showPw ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}</button>
              </div>
              {password && <div className="strength"><div style={{ width: `${st.pct}%`, background: st.color }} /></div>}
              <span className="field-error">{errors.password}</span>
            </div>
            <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner" /> : 'Create account'}</button>
          </form>
        )}

        {tab === 'otp' && (
          <form onSubmit={submitOtp} noValidate>
            <button type="button" className="btn-ghost small" onClick={() => switchTab('login')}>← Back</button>
            <h2 style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>Verify your email</h2>
            <p className="muted small">We sent a 6-digit code to <b>{masked}</b>. It expires in 5 minutes.</p>
            <div className="otp-row" onPaste={onPaste}>
              {digits.map((d, i) => (
                <input
                  key={i}
                  ref={(el) => { otpRefs.current[i] = el; }}
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  maxLength={1}
                  value={d}
                  aria-label={`Digit ${i + 1}`}
                  onChange={(e) => onDigit(i, e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Backspace' && !digits[i] && i > 0 && otpRefs.current[i - 1]?.focus()}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
            <button className="btn btn-primary btn-block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? <span className="spinner" /> : 'Verify & continue'}</button>
            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 14 }} className="small muted">
              Didn’t get it?{' '}
              <button type="button" onClick={resend} disabled={countdown > 0} style={{ color: countdown > 0 ? undefined : 'var(--accent)', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                Resend{countdown > 0 ? ` (${countdown}s)` : ''}
              </button>
            </p>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export type IconName = keyof typeof PATHS;

type Props = { name: IconName; size?: number; strokeWidth?: number } & Omit<SVGProps<SVGSVGElement>, 'name'>;

export default function Icon({ name, size = 20, strokeWidth = 1.5, ...rest }: Props) {
  return (
    <svg
      width={size}
      height={size}
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
      focusable="false"
      {...rest}
    >
      {PATHS[name]}
    </svg>
  );
}

'use client';

import {
  BarElement, CategoryScale, Chart as ChartJS, Filler, LinearScale, LineElement, PointElement, Tooltip, Legend,
} from 'chart.js';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Bar, Line } from 'react-chartjs-2';

ChartJS.register(CategoryScale, LinearScale, PointElement, LineElement, BarElement, Filler, Tooltip, Legend);

const TEAL = '#14B8A6';
const MUTED = '#8B98A5';
const GRID = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';

const tooltip = {
  backgroundColor: '#111A22', borderColor: '#1E2A35', borderWidth: 1, titleColor: '#E6EDF3',
  bodyColor: '#8B98A5', padding: 10, cornerRadius: 8, displayColors: false,
};

/** Canvas can't read CSS variables, so resolve the real (next/font) family from the DOM before drawing. */
function useChartBox(height: number) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [font, setFont] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [reduce, setReduce] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ref.current) setFont(getComputedStyle(ref.current).fontFamily);
    setReduce(window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);
  }, []);
  const box = (children: React.ReactNode) => (
    <div className="chart-box" ref={ref} style={{ height }}>{font ? children : null}</div>
  );
  return { box, font: font || 'system-ui', reduce };
}

// Chart.js "progressive line" so the line draws in left to right.
function drawIn(n: number) {
  const total = 900;
  const step = total / Math.max(1, n);
  const prevY = (ctx: any) =>
    ctx.index === 0
      ? ctx.chart.scales.y.getPixelForValue(ctx.chart.scales.y.min)
      : ctx.chart.getDatasetMeta(ctx.datasetIndex).data[ctx.index - 1].getProps(['y'], true).y;
  return {
    x: {
      type: 'number', easing: 'linear', duration: step, from: NaN,
      delay(ctx: any) { if (ctx.type !== 'data' || ctx.xStarted) return 0; ctx.xStarted = true; return ctx.index * step; },
    },
    y: {
      type: 'number', easing: 'linear', duration: step, from: prevY,
      delay(ctx: any) { if (ctx.type !== 'data' || ctx.yStarted) return 0; ctx.yStarted = true; return ctx.index * step; },
    },
  } as any;
}

export function AccuracyLine({
  labels, data, label = 'Accuracy', height = 260, zoom = false, highlightLast = false,
}: { labels: string[]; data: number[]; label?: string; height?: number; zoom?: boolean; highlightLast?: boolean }) {
  const { box, font, reduce } = useChartBox(height);
  const min = zoom && data.length ? Math.max(0, Math.floor((Math.min(...data) - 5) / 10) * 10) : 0;
  const lastIdx = data.length - 1;
  const isLast = (i: number) => highlightLast && i === lastIdx;
  return box(
    <Line
      data={{
        labels,
        datasets: [{
          label, data, borderColor: TEAL, backgroundColor: 'rgba(20,184,166,0.10)',
          pointBackgroundColor: highlightLast ? data.map((_, i) => (isLast(i) ? '#FFFFFF' : TEAL)) : TEAL,
          pointBorderColor: TEAL,
          pointBorderWidth: highlightLast ? data.map((_, i) => (isLast(i) ? 2.5 : 1)) : 1,
          fill: true, tension: 0.35,
          pointRadius: highlightLast ? data.map((_, i) => (isLast(i) ? 5 : 3)) : data.length > 30 ? 0 : 3,
          pointHoverRadius: 6, borderWidth: 2,
        }],
      }}
      options={{
        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
        animation: reduce ? false : drawIn(data.length),
        font: { family: font },
        color: MUTED,
        plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: { ...tooltip, callbacks: { label: (c) => ` ${c.parsed.y}%` } } },
        scales: {
          x: { grid: { display: false }, border: { display: false }, ticks: { color: MUTED, maxTicksLimit: 7 } },
          y: { min, max: 100, grid: { color: GRID }, border: { display: false }, ticks: { color: MUTED, callback: (v) => `${v}%` } },
        },
      }}
    />,
  );
}

export function CountBars({
  labels, data, label = 'Sessions', height = 260,
}: { labels: string[]; data: number[]; label?: string; height?: number }) {
  const { box, font, reduce } = useChartBox(height);
  return box(
    <Bar
      data={{ labels, datasets: [{ label, data, backgroundColor: TEAL, hoverBackgroundColor: '#19C6B3', borderRadius: 4, maxBarThickness: 28 }] }}
      options={{
        responsive: true, maintainAspectRatio: false,
        animation: reduce ? false : { duration: 700, easing: 'easeOutQuart' },
        font: { family: font },
        color: MUTED,
        plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip },
        scales: {
          x: { grid: { display: false }, border: { display: false }, ticks: { color: MUTED, maxTicksLimit: 7 } },
          y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { color: GRID }, border: { display: false }, ticks: { color: MUTED, precision: 0 } },
        },
      }}
    />,
  );
}

FILE: frontend/components/ui.tsx

'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { CSSProperties, ReactNode } from 'react';
import Icon, { IconName } from './Icon';

const cx = (...a: (string | false | null | undefined)[]) => a.filter(Boolean).join(' ');

/* ── PageHeader ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function PageHeader({ title, subtitle, actions }: { title: string; subtitle?: string; actions?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="page-header">
      <div className="ph-text">
        <h1>{title}</h1>
        {subtitle && <p>{subtitle}</p>}
      </div>
      {actions && <div className="ph-actions">{actions}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Card / CardTitle ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function Card({
  children, className, style, padded = true, as: Tag = 'section', ...rest
}: {
  children: ReactNode; className?: string; style?: CSSProperties; padded?: boolean; as?: 'section' | 'div' | 'article';
} & React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>) {
  const T = Tag as 'section';
  return <T className={cx('ui-card', !padded && 'flush', className)} style={style} {...rest}>{children}</T>;
}

export function CardTitle({ icon, children, action }: { icon?: IconName; children: ReactNode; action?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="card-title">
      <h2>{icon && <Icon name={icon} size={20} />}{children}</h2>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── SegmentedControl (sliding pill) ────────────────────────────────────── */
export function SegmentedControl<T extends string | number>({
  options, value, onChange, ariaLabel,
}: {
  options: { value: T; label: ReactNode }[]; value: T; onChange: (v: T) => void; ariaLabel: string;
}) {
  const idx = Math.max(0, options.findIndex((o) => o.value === value));
  return (
    <div
      className="segmented"
      role="radiogroup"
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      style={{ '--n': options.length, '--idx': idx } as CSSProperties}
    >
      <span className="seg-pill" aria-hidden="true" />
      {options.map((o) => (
        <button
          key={String(o.value)}
          type="button"
          role="radio"
          aria-checked={o.value === value}
          className={o.value === value ? 'on' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange(o.value)}
        >
          {o.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Chip ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export type Tone = 'neutral' | 'green' | 'amber' | 'red' | 'teal';
export function Chip({ tone = 'neutral', icon, dot, children, className }: {
  tone?: Tone; icon?: IconName; dot?: boolean; children: ReactNode; className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <span className={cx('ui-chip', `tone-${tone}`, className)}>
      {dot && <i className="chip-dot" />}
      {icon && <Icon name={icon} size={14} />}
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

/** Map a 0-100 score to a chip tone. */
export const scoreTone = (n: number): Tone => (n >= 80 ? 'green' : n >= 60 ? 'amber' : 'red');

/* ── EmptyState ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function EmptyState({ icon, title, message, action }: { icon: IconName; title?: string; message: string; action?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="empty-state">
      <span className="es-icon"><Icon name={icon} size={24} /></span>
      {title && <strong>{title}</strong>}
      <p>{message}</p>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ── Skeleton ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function Skeleton({ width = '100%', height = 16, radius = 8, className }: {
  width?: number | string; height?: number | string; radius?: number; className?: string;
}) {
  return <span className={cx('skeleton', className)} style={{ width, height, borderRadius: radius }} aria-hidden="true" />;
}

/* ── Count-up (once, on first value) ────────────────────────────────────── */
export function useCountUp(target: number | null | undefined, duration = 700) {
  const [val, setVal] = useState(0);
  const done = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (target == null) return;
    const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    if (done.current || reduce) { setVal(target); done.current = true; return; }
    done.current = true;
    const start = performance.now();
    let raf = 0;
    const tick = (now: number) => {
      const t = Math.min(1, (now - start) / duration);
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
      setVal(target * eased);
      if (t < 1) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick); else setVal(target);
    };
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [target, duration]);
  return val;
}

export function CountUp({ value, decimals = 0, suffix = '' }: { value: number | null | undefined; decimals?: number; suffix?: string }) {
  const v = useCountUp(value);
  if (value == null) return <>—</>;
  return <>{v.toFixed(decimals)}{suffix}</>;
}

/* ── StatCard ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function StatCard({ label, value, decimals = 0, suffix, hint, icon, loading }: {
  label: string; value: number | string | null | undefined; decimals?: number; suffix?: string;
  hint?: string; icon?: IconName; loading?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <Card className="stat-card">
      <div className="stat-label">{icon && <Icon name={icon} size={16} />}{label}</div>
      {loading ? (
        <Skeleton width={96} height={32} />
      ) : (
        <div className="stat-value">
          {typeof value === 'number' ? <CountUp value={value} decimals={decimals} suffix={suffix} /> : value ?? '—'}
        </div>
      )}
      {hint && <div className="stat-hint">{hint}</div>}
    </Card>
  );
}

/* ── Avatar ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function Avatar({ name, size = 32 }: { name?: string | null; size?: number }) {
  return (
    <span className="ui-avatar" style={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: Math.round(size * 0.42) }} aria-hidden="true">
      {(name || '?').trim()[0]?.toUpperCase() || '?'}
    </span>
  );
}

/* ── Switch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
export function Switch({ checked, onChange, label }: { checked: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void; label: string }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" role="switch" aria-checked={checked} className="switch-row" onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <span className={cx('switch', checked && 'on')} aria-hidden="true"><i /></span>
    </button>
  );
}

========================================================================
FILE: frontend/lib/api.ts
========================================================================
import { API_URL } from './config';

// Access token lives in memory only. The refresh token is an httpOnly cookie.
let accessToken: string | null = null;

export const getToken = () => accessToken;
export const setToken = (t: string | null) => { accessToken = t; };

export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  code?: string;
  constructor(message: string, status: number, code?: string) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
  }
}

// Concurrent callers (React strict-mode double effects, parallel requests) share ONE refresh call.
// Refresh tokens rotate, so two simultaneous refreshes would race and could revoke the session.
let refreshing: Promise<boolean> | null = null;

function refresh(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!refreshing) {
    refreshing = (async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${API_URL}/auth/refresh`, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' });
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (res.ok && data.data?.accessToken) { accessToken = data.data.accessToken; return true; }
      } catch { /* network error */ }
      return false;
    })().finally(() => { refreshing = null; });
  }
  return refreshing;
}

const tokenExpiresInMs = (t: string): number => {
  try {
    const payload = JSON.parse(atob(t.split('.')[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')));
    return payload.exp * 1000 - Date.now();
  } catch { return 0; }
};

/** A token that is valid for at least another minute (refreshes if needed). Used for the socket handshake. */
export async function getFreshToken(): Promise<string | null> {
  if (!accessToken || tokenExpiresInMs(accessToken) < 60_000) await refresh();
  return accessToken;
}

// Pick the most useful message from the backend's error shapes.
const errorMessage = (data: any, status: number): string => {
  if (typeof data?.error === 'string' && data.error) return data.error;
  if (Array.isArray(data?.details) && data.details[0]?.msg) return data.details[0].msg;
  if (status === 429) return 'Too many requests. Please wait a moment and try again.';
  return `HTTP ${status}`;
};

async function request<T = any>(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown, retried = false): Promise<T> {
  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await fetch(`${API_URL}${path}`, {
      method,
      credentials: 'include',
      headers: {
        ...(body !== undefined && method !== 'GET' ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}),
        ...(accessToken ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` } : {}),
      },
      body: body !== undefined && method !== 'GET' ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
    });
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError('Cannot reach the server. Check your connection and try again.', 0, 'NETWORK_ERROR');
  }

  // Try a silent refresh on 401 — but never for /auth/* calls: a wrong password / wrong OTP is a
  // legitimate 401 and must not trigger (and rotate) a refresh, then replay the request.
  if (res.status === 401 && !retried && !path.startsWith('/auth/') && (await refresh())) {
    return request<T>(method, path, body, true);
  }

  const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(errorMessage(data, res.status), res.status, data.code);
  return data;
}

// Login / OTP / Google responses carry the access token; keep it in memory right away.
const withToken = async (p: Promise<any>) => {
  const res = await p;
  if (res?.data?.accessToken) accessToken = res.data.accessToken;
  return res;
};

const qs = (p: Record<string, any> = {}) => {
  const s = new URLSearchParams(Object.entries(p).filter(([, v]) => v !== '' && v != null) as any).toString();
  return s ? `?${s}` : '';
};

export const api = {
  bootstrap: async () => (accessToken ? true : refresh()),
  auth: {
    register: (d: any) => request('POST', '/auth/register', d),
    login: (d: any) => withToken(request('POST', '/auth/login', d)),
    logout: () => request('POST', '/auth/logout', {}),
    google: (idToken: string) => withToken(request('POST', '/auth/google', { idToken })),
    verifyOtp: (d: any) => withToken(request('POST', '/auth/verify-otp', d)),
    resendOtp: (d: any) => request('POST', '/auth/resend-otp', d),
    forgotPassword: (d: any) => request('POST', '/auth/forgot-password', d),
    resetPassword: (token: string, d: any) => request('POST', `/auth/reset-password/${token}`, d),
  },
  users: {
    me: () => request('GET', '/users/me'),
    updateProfile: (d: any) => request('PATCH', '/users/profile', d),
    leaderboard: () => request('GET', '/users/leaderboard'),
  },
  sessions: {
    list: (p: Record<string, any> = {}) => request('GET', `/sessions${qs(p)}`),
    create: (d: any) => request('POST', '/sessions', d),
    remove: (id: string) => request('DELETE', `/sessions/${id}`),
  },
  speech: {
    analyze: (d: any) => request('POST', '/speech/analyze', d),
    accuracyTrend: (days = 30) => request('GET', `/speech/accuracy-trend?days=${days}`),
  },
  chat: {
    messages: (roomId = 'global', page = 1) => request('GET', `/chat/messages${qs({ roomId, page })}`),
  },
  phrases: {
    daily: (lang: string, difficulty: string, refreshNow = false) =>
      request('GET', `/phrases/daily${qs({ lang, difficulty, refresh: refreshNow ? '1' : '' })}`),
  },
  conversation: {
    chat: (d: any) => request('POST', '/conversation/chat', d),
  },
};

export type User = {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  role?: string;
  emailVerified?: boolean;
  avatar?: string | null;
  profile?: { nativeLanguage?: string; targetLanguages?: string[]; totalSessions?: number; totalMinutes?: number; avgAccuracy?: number };
  activity?: { streak?: { current?: number; longest?: number }; totalSessions?: number; totalMinutes?: number } | null;
};

FILE: frontend/lib/auth-context.tsx

'use client';

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api, setToken, User } from './api';

type AuthState = {
  user: User | null;
  loading: boolean;
  reload: () => Promise<User | null>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
};

const Ctx = createContext<AuthState>({ user: null, loading: true, reload: async () => null, logout: async () => {} });
export const useAuth = () => useContext(Ctx);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      if (!(await api.bootstrap())) throw new Error('no session');
      const res = await api.users.me();
      setUser(res.data.user);
      return res.data.user as User;
    } catch {
      setUser(null);
      return null;
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { reload(); }, [reload]);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    try { await api.auth.logout(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    setToken(null);
    setUser(null);
    window.location.href = '/';
  }, []);

  return <Ctx.Provider value={{ user, loading, reload, logout }}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

'use client';

// Firebase client SDK — used ONLY for Google sign-in.
// The app's own session (JWT cookies) is still issued by the backend after it verifies the ID token.
//
// Everything is initialised lazily inside getGoogleIdToken(). Initialising at import time made the
// whole landing page crash (and `next build` fail) whenever NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_* was missing,
// even for people who only use email/password.

import { initializeApp, getApps, getApp } from 'firebase/app';
import { getAuth, GoogleAuthProvider, signInWithPopup, signOut } from 'firebase/auth';

const firebaseConfig = {
  apiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY,
  authDomain: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN,
  projectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
  appId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID,
};

/** Opens the Google popup and returns a Firebase ID token for the backend to verify. */
export async function getGoogleIdToken(): Promise<string> {
  if (!firebaseConfig.apiKey || !firebaseConfig.projectId) {
    throw new Error('Google sign-in is not configured. Set the NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_* variables.');
  }
  const app = getApps().length ? getApp() : initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
  const auth = getAuth(app);

  const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
  provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });
  const cred = await signInWithPopup(auth, provider);
  const idToken = await cred.user.getIdToken();
  // We use our own backend session, so don't keep a second (Firebase) session in the browser.
  await signOut(auth).catch(() => { /* ignore */ });
  return idToken;
}

'use client';

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useState } from 'react';

type Toast = { id: number; msg: string; type: 'success' | 'error' | 'info' };
const Ctx = createContext<(msg: string, type?: Toast['type']) => void>(() => {});
export const useToast = () => useContext(Ctx);

export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<Toast[]>([]);
  const push = useCallback((msg: string, type: Toast['type'] = 'info') => {
    const id = Date.now() + Math.random();
    setItems((s) => [...s, { id, msg, type }]);
    setTimeout(() => setItems((s) => s.filter((t) => t.id !== id)), 4000);
  }, []);
  return (
    <Ctx.Provider value={push}>
      {children}
      <div className="toasts" aria-live="polite">
        {items.map((t) => (
          <div key={t.id} className={`toast ${t.type}`}>{t.msg}</div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </Ctx.Provider>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/lib/useSpeech.ts
========================================================================
'use client';

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

type Options = { lang: string; continuous?: boolean; onFinal?: (text: string) => void };

/** Thin wrapper around the browser Web Speech API. */
export function useSpeechRecognition({ lang, continuous = false, onFinal }: Options) {
  const [supported, setSupported] = useState(true);
  const [listening, setListening] = useState(false);
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const recRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const wantRef = useRef(false);
  const finalRef = useRef('');
  const onFinalRef = useRef(onFinal);
  onFinalRef.current = onFinal;

  useEffect(() => {
    const w = window as any;
    const ios = /iPad|iPhone|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent);
    setSupported(!!(w.SpeechRecognition || w.webkitSpeechRecognition) && !ios);
  }, []);

  const stop = useCallback(() => {
    wantRef.current = false;
    try { recRef.current?.stop(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    setListening(false);
  }, []);

  const start = useCallback(async (): Promise<string | null> => {
    const w = window as any;
    const SR = w.SpeechRecognition || w.webkitSpeechRecognition;
    if (!SR) return 'unsupported';
    finalRef.current = '';
    setText('');
    const rec = new SR();
    rec.lang = lang;
    rec.continuous = continuous;
    rec.interimResults = true;
    let error: string | null = null;
    rec.onresult = (e: any) => {
      let interim = '';
      for (let i = e.resultIndex; i < e.results.length; i++) {
        const t = e.results[i][0].transcript;
        if (e.results[i].isFinal) {
          finalRef.current = (finalRef.current + ' ' + t).trim();
          onFinalRef.current?.(t.trim());
        } else interim += t;
      }
      setText((finalRef.current + ' ' + interim).trim());
    };
    rec.onerror = (e: any) => {
      if (e.error === 'no-speech' || e.error === 'aborted') return;
      error = e.error;
      wantRef.current = false;
      setListening(false);
    };
    rec.onend = () => {
      if (wantRef.current && continuous) {
        try { rec.start(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
      } else {
        setListening(false);
      }
    };
    recRef.current = rec;
    wantRef.current = true;
    try { rec.start(); setListening(true); } catch { return 'failed'; }
    return error;
  }, [lang, continuous]);

  useEffect(() => () => { wantRef.current = false; try { recRef.current?.abort(); } catch { /* ignore */ } }, []);

  return { supported, listening, text, setText, start, stop };
}

        value: https://linguawave-frontend.onrender.com
